<script id="ethvelle-v150-final-fixes-runtime">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV150FinalFixes)return;
  window.__ethvelleV150FinalFixes=true;

  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const activeChar=()=>{try{return typeof activeCharacter==='function'?activeCharacter():null;}catch(_e){return null;}};
  const esc=v=>String(v==null?'':v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
  const ensureProps=char=>{
    if(!char)return null;
    if(!char.handProps||typeof char.handProps!=='object')char.handProps={L:null,R:null};
    if(!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(char.handProps,'L'))char.handProps.L=null;
    if(!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(char.handProps,'R'))char.handProps.R=null;
    return char.handProps;
  };
  const sameLegacyMirror=(a,b)=>{
    if(!a||!b||!a.src||!b.src||a.src!==b.src)return false;
    const val=(o,k,d)=>o[k]===undefined?d:o[k];
    return ['name','scale','rotation','offsetX','offsetY','flipX','flipY'].every(k=>val(a,k,({scale:1,rotation:0,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,flipX:false,flipY:false})[k])===val(b,k,({scale:1,rotation:0,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,flipX:false,flipY:false})[k]));
  };
  function removeAccidentalMirror(char){
    const p=ensureProps(char);if(!p||!sameLegacyMirror(p.L,p.R))return false;
    /* v148 always uploaded into Right Hand and cloned that same record into Left Hand. */
    p.L=null;return true;
  }
  /* Migrate an old v148 mirror once per character. Drawing must stay read-only so a
     user can intentionally place the same image in both hands after the migration. */
  const mirrorMigrationDone=typeof WeakSet==='function'?new WeakSet():null;
  function cleanupLegacyMirrorOnce(char){
    if(!char)return false;
    if(mirrorMigrationDone&&mirrorMigrationDone.has(char))return false;
    if(mirrorMigrationDone)mirrorMigrationDone.add(char);
    const changed=removeAccidentalMirror(char);
    if(changed&&typeof persistProjectState==='function')try{persistProjectState();}catch(_e){}
    return changed;
  }
  function getSide(back){
    const row=back&&back.querySelector('.evpa-action-side');if(!row)return 'R';
    const buttons=Array.from(row.querySelectorAll('button'));
    const left=buttons.find(b=>/left/i.test(b.textContent||'')),right=buttons.find(b=>/right/i.test(b.textContent||''));
    if(left&&left.classList.contains('active'))return 'L';
    if(right&&right.classList.contains('active'))return 'R';
    return 'R';
  }
  function updateHeldPanelUI(char,side){
    const back=$('ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop');if(!back)return;
    const props=ensureProps(char),item=props&&props[side]||null;
    const sides=back.querySelectorAll('.evpa-action-side button');
    sides.forEach((button,index)=>{
      const active=(index===0?'L':'R')===side;
      button.classList.toggle('active',active);button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',active?'true':'false');
    });
    const preview=back.querySelector('#evpaPropPreview');
    if(preview)preview.innerHTML=item&&item.src?'<img src="'+esc(item.src)+'" alt="Object image"><button type="button" class="evpa-prop-remove-icon" aria-label="Remove held object" title="Remove object" onclick="removeCharacterHeldProp()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 7h16M9 7V4h6v3m-9 0 1 13h10l1-13M10 11v5m4-5v5"/></svg></button>':'<span class="evpa-prop-empty">No object added</span>';
    const upload=back.querySelector('.evpa-prop-upload');if(upload)upload.textContent='＋ '+(item&&item.src?'Replace object':'Add object image');
    const controls=back.querySelector('.evpa-prop-controls');
    if(controls){controls.style.opacity=item&&item.src?'1':'.45';controls.style.pointerEvents=item&&item.src?'auto':'none';}
    const values=[item?Number(item.scale)||1:1,item?Number(item.rotation)||0:0,item?Number(item.offsetX)||0:0,item?Number(item.offsetY)||0:0];
    const ranges=controls?Array.from(controls.querySelectorAll('input[type="range"]')):[];
    ranges.slice(0,4).forEach((range,i)=>{range.value=String(i===0?Math.round(values[i]*100):Math.round(values[i]));});
    const ids=['evpaPropScaleVal','evpaPropRotationVal','evpaPropXVal','evpaPropYVal'];
    ids.forEach((id,i)=>{const out=$(id);if(out)out.textContent=i===0?Math.round(values[i]*100)+'%':i===1?Math.round(values[i])+'°':String(Math.round(values[i]));});
    const flip=back.querySelector('.evpa-v149-flip-row');
    if(flip){flip.style.opacity=item&&item.src?'1':'.45';flip.style.pointerEvents=item&&item.src?'auto':'none';flip.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(btn=>{const on=!!(item&&item[btn.dataset.flipField]);btn.classList.toggle('active',on);btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed',on?'true':'false');});}
  }

  /* Block the v148 auto-mirror logic by temporarily making both hand slots non-empty
     while its wrappers call the original panel/side handlers. */
  function callWithMirrorGuard(char,fn){
    const p=ensureProps(char),guards=[];
    if(p){['L','R'].forEach(side=>{if(!p[side]){const token={src:'',__ethvelleV150MirrorGuard:true};p[side]=token;guards.push({side,token});}});}
    let result;
    try{result=fn();}finally{guards.forEach(g=>{if(p[g.side]===g.token)p[g.side]=null;});}
    return result;
  }
  const priorOpenHeld=window.openCharacterPropsPanel;
  if(typeof priorOpenHeld==='function'){
    window.openCharacterPropsPanel=function(){
      const char=activeChar();cleanupLegacyMirrorOnce(char);
      const result=callWithMirrorGuard(char,()=>priorOpenHeld.apply(this,arguments));
      if(char)updateHeldPanelUI(char,'R');
      return result;
    };
  }
  const priorSetSide=window.setCharacterPropSide;
  function safeSetSide(side){
    if(side!=='L'&&side!=='R')return;
    const char=activeChar();
    const result=callWithMirrorGuard(char,()=>priorSetSide&&priorSetSide.call(window,side));
    if(char)updateHeldPanelUI(char,side);
    return result;
  }
  if(typeof priorSetSide==='function')window.setCharacterPropSide=function(side){return safeSetSide(side);};
  function refreshStageSide(side){
    const other=side==='L'?'R':'L';
    /* Two genuine side changes refresh the high-resolution preview using the v130 renderer. */
    safeSetSide(other);safeSetSide(side);
  }

  /* Migrate identical L/R records created by the old mirror feature and keep drawing
     strictly to the side records the user actually chose. */
  let migrated=false;
  if(typeof characters!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(characters))characters.forEach(char=>{if(cleanupLegacyMirrorOnce(char))migrated=true;});
  if(migrated&&typeof window.persistProjectState==='function')try{window.persistProjectState();}catch(_e){}

  window.setCharacterHeldPropFile=function(input){
    const file=input&&input.files&&input.files[0];if(input)input.value='';if(!file)return;
    const char=activeChar();if(!char)return;
    cleanupLegacyMirrorOnce(char);
    const side=getSide($('ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop'));
    safeSetSide(side);
    const reader=new FileReader();
    reader.onerror=()=>{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not read the object image',{icon:toastIcons.info});};
    reader.onload=()=>{
      const img=new Image();
      img.onerror=()=>{if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not decode the object image',{icon:toastIcons.info});};
      img.onload=()=>{
        const max=640,ratio=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.naturalWidth,img.naturalHeight));
        const w=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalWidth*ratio)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalHeight*ratio));
        const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=w;canvas.height=h;
        const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');if(!ctx){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Object image could not be prepared',{icon:toastIcons.info});return;}
        ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
        const props=ensureProps(char);
        props[side]={src:canvas.toDataURL('image/png'),name:file.name||'Object',scale:1,rotation:0,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,flipX:false,flipY:false};
        const clip=typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&typeof getClipByRef==='function'?getClipByRef(selectedClip):null;
        const parts=char.sheet&&char.sheet.parts||{},field=side==='L'?'leftHand':'rightHand';
        if(clip&&parts['hand'+side+'_hold']){const a={expression:'none',mouth:'preset',blink:'auto',eyes:'auto',leftHand:'none',rightHand:'none',...(clip.faceActions&&typeof clip.faceActions==='object'?clip.faceActions:{})};clip.faceActions={...a,[field]:'hold'};}
        if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();
        if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();
        updateHeldPanelUI(char,side);refreshStageSide(side);
        if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Object attached at the '+(side==='L'?'left':'right')+' hand',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1400});
      };
      img.src=String(reader.result||'');
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };

  window.removeCharacterHeldProp=function(){
    const char=activeChar();if(!char)return;
    const side=getSide($('ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop')),props=ensureProps(char);
    safeSetSide(side);props[side]=null;
    if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();
    updateHeldPanelUI(char,side);refreshStageSide(side);
  };

  /* Do not call the old v148-wrapped draw hook: that hook silently clones a lone prop
     into the opposite hand before painting. */
  window.ethvelleDrawCharacterProps=function(ctx,char,boneStart,boneEnd){
    const props=char&&char.handProps,cache=window.__ethvelleHeldPropImageCache||(window.__ethvelleHeldPropImageCache=new Map());
    if(props){['L','R'].forEach(side=>{
      const item=props[side];if(!item||!item.src)return;
      let img=cache.get(item.src);
      if(!img){img=new Image();img.onload=()=>{if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();};img.onerror=()=>{};cache.set(item.src,img);img.src=item.src;}
      if(!img.complete||!img.naturalWidth)return;
      let start,end;try{start=boneStart('hand'+side);end=boneEnd('hand'+side);}catch(_e){return;}
      if(!start||!end||![start.x,start.y,end.x,end.y].every(Number.isFinite))return;
      const dx=end.x-start.x,dy=end.y-start.y,angle=Math.atan2(dy,dx)-Math.PI/2+(Number(item.rotation)||0)*Math.PI/180;
      const handX=start.x+dx*.5,handY=start.y+dy*.5;
      const bodyKeys=['spine_lower','spine_upper','chest','neck','thighL','shinL','thighR','shinR'];
      const bodyLen=bodyKeys.reduce((sum,key)=>sum+Math.max(0,Number(char.bones&&char.bones[key]&&char.bones[key].length)||0),0)||Number(typeof stageH!=='undefined'?stageH:420)||420;
      const scale=Math.max(.2,Math.min(2.5,Number(item.scale)||1));
      const rawH=Math.max(.5,bodyLen*.235*scale),aspect=img.naturalWidth/Math.max(1,img.naturalHeight),rawW=rawH*aspect;
      const fit=Math.min(1,320/Math.max(rawH,rawW)),h=rawH*fit,w=rawW*fit;
      ctx.save();ctx.translate(handX,handY);ctx.rotate(angle);ctx.translate(Number(item.offsetX)||0,Number(item.offsetY)||0);
      ctx.scale(item.flipX?-1:1,item.flipY?-1:1);ctx.drawImage(img,-w*.5,-h*.5,w,h);ctx.restore();
    });}
    const mouth=char&&char.mouthProp,type=String(char&&(char.modelType||(char.sheet&&char.sheet.modelType))||'humanoid');
    if(mouth&&mouth.src&&(type==='animal'||type==='bird')){
      let img=cache.get(mouth.src);
      if(!img){img=new Image();img.onload=()=>{if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();};img.onerror=()=>{};cache.set(mouth.src,img);img.src=mouth.src;}
      if(img.complete&&img.naturalWidth){
        let start,end;try{start=boneStart('head');end=boneEnd('head');}catch(_e){start=end=null;}
        if(start&&end&&[start.x,start.y,end.x,end.y].every(Number.isFinite)){
          const dx=end.x-start.x,dy=end.y-start.y,headLen=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
          const bodyKeys=['spine_lower','spine_upper','chest','neck','thighL','shinL','thighR','shinR'];
          const bodyLen=bodyKeys.reduce((sum,key)=>sum+Math.max(0,Number(char.bones&&char.bones[key]&&char.bones[key].length)||0),0)||Number(typeof stageH!=='undefined'?stageH:420)||420;
          const frontX=-dy/headLen,frontY=dx/headLen;
          const anchorX=start.x+dx*.55+frontX*bodyLen*.045,anchorY=start.y+dy*.55+frontY*bodyLen*.045;
          const angle=Math.atan2(dy,dx)+Math.PI/2+(Number(mouth.rotation)||0)*Math.PI/180;
          const scale=Math.max(.2,Math.min(2.2,Number(mouth.scale)||1)),rawH=Math.max(12,bodyLen*.12*scale),aspect=img.naturalWidth/Math.max(1,img.naturalHeight),rawW=rawH*aspect;
          const fit=Math.min(1,180/Math.max(rawH,rawW)),h=rawH*fit,w=rawW*fit;
          ctx.save();ctx.translate(anchorX,anchorY);ctx.rotate(angle);ctx.translate(Number(mouth.offsetX)||0,Number(mouth.offsetY)||0);ctx.scale(mouth.flipX?-1:1,mouth.flipY?-1:1);ctx.drawImage(img,-w*.5,-h*.5,w,h);ctx.restore();
        }
      }
    }
  };

  /* Custom face/hand variants become selectable in the same grid as the normal model parts. */
  function fieldForVariant(category,item){
    if(category==='expressions'&&item.kind==='custom-expression')return 'customExpressionId';
    if(category==='headParts'){
      if(item.kind==='custom-eyes')return 'customEyeId';
      if(item.kind==='mouth-open')return 'customMouthOpenId';
      if(item.kind==='mouth-closed')return 'customMouthClosedId';
    }
    if(category==='gestures')return item.side==='L'||item.kind==='left-hand-gesture'?'customGestureLId':'customGestureRId';
    return null;
  }
  function targetEditorChar(){
    try{if(typeof ED!=='undefined'&&ED&&ED.kind==='edit'&&Number.isInteger(ED.editIdx)&&characters[ED.editIdx])return characters[ED.editIdx];}catch(_e){}
    return activeChar();
  }
  function applyVariantToCharacter(item,field){
    const char=targetEditorChar();if(!char||!char.sheet||!item||!field)return;
    if(!char.expr||typeof char.expr!=='object')char.expr=typeof sheetExprFresh==='function'?sheetExprFresh():{};
    const expr=char.expr,id=String(item.id);
    if(field==='customExpressionId'){
      expr.customExpressionId=id;expr.customEyeId=null;expr.customMouthOpenId=null;expr.customMouthClosedId=null;expr.emo=null;
    }else if(field==='customEyeId'){
      expr.customEyeId=id;expr.customExpressionId=null;
    }else if(field==='customMouthOpenId'){
      expr.customMouthOpenId=id;expr.customMouthClosedId=null;expr.mouth='open';
    }else if(field==='customMouthClosedId'){
      expr.customMouthClosedId=id;expr.customMouthOpenId=null;expr.mouth='closed';
    }else if(field==='customGestureLId'){
      expr.customGestureLId=id;expr.gestL=null;expr.fistL=false;
    }else if(field==='customGestureRId'){
      expr.customGestureRId=id;expr.gestR=null;expr.fistR=false;
    }
    if(char.sheet&&typeof ED!=='undefined'&&ED.extraVariants){
      char.sheet.customVariants=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ED.extraVariants));
    }
    const clip=typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&typeof getClipByRef==='function'?getClipByRef(selectedClip):null;
    if(clip){
      const a={...(clip.faceActions&&typeof clip.faceActions==='object'?clip.faceActions:{})};a[field]=id;
      if(field==='customExpressionId'){a.expression='none';a.customEyeId=null;a.customMouthOpenId=null;a.customMouthClosedId=null;}
      else if(field==='customEyeId'){a.eyes='closed';a.expression='none';a.customExpressionId=null;}
      else if(field==='customMouthOpenId'){a.mouth='talk';a.customMouthClosedId=null;}
      else if(field==='customMouthClosedId'){a.mouth='closed';a.customMouthOpenId=null;}
      else if(field==='customGestureLId')a.leftHand='none';
      else if(field==='customGestureRId')a.rightHand='none';
      clip.faceActions=a;
    }
    if(typeof pausePlayback==='function')pausePlayback();
    if(typeof renderFrame==='function'&&clip)renderFrame();
    if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();
    if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();
  }
  function renderEditorVariantTiles(){
    const box=$('edLayers');if(!box||typeof ED==='undefined'||!ED)return;
    box.querySelectorAll('.ev150-variant-chip,.ev150-custom-upload-tile').forEach(node=>node.remove());
    const variants=ED.extraVariants||{},active=window.__ethvelleV150ActiveVariant||null;
    ['expressions','headParts','gestures'].forEach(category=>{
      const list=Array.isArray(variants[category])?variants[category]:[];
      list.forEach(item=>{
        const field=fieldForVariant(category,item);if(!field||!item||!item.id||!item.src)return;
        const button=document.createElement('button');button.type='button';
        const selected=!!(active&&active.id===item.id&&active.field===field);
        const displayName=String(item.fileName||item.originalFileName||item.name||'Custom part');
        button.className='ed-chip ev150-variant-chip'+(selected?' is-on':'');
        button.dataset.variantId=String(item.id);button.dataset.variantField=field;
        button.title=displayName;
        button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',selected?'true':'false');
        button.innerHTML='<span class="th"><img src="'+esc(item.src)+'" alt=""></span><span class="n">'+esc(displayName)+'</span>';
        button.addEventListener('click',event=>{
          event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();
          window.__ethvelleV150ActiveVariant={id:String(item.id),field};
          if(typeof ED!=='undefined'){
            ED.sel=null;
            const targetKey=field==='customGestureLId'?(ED.st.handL?'handL':'armL_lower'):field==='customGestureRId'?(ED.st.handR?'handR':'armR_lower'):'head';
            ED.previewVariant={id:String(item.id),field,src:item.src,targetKey};
          }
          applyVariantToCharacter(item,field);
          if(typeof window.edSyncUI==='function')window.edSyncUI();
          if($('edSubtitle'))$('edSubtitle').textContent=displayName+' selected';
          if(typeof edDraw==='function')edDraw();
        });
        box.appendChild(button);
      });
    });
    if(active&&$('edSubtitle'))$('edSubtitle').textContent='Custom part selected';
  }
  const priorEdSync=window.edSyncUI;
  if(typeof priorEdSync==='function'){
    window.edSyncUI=function(){const result=priorEdSync.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(()=>{const old=$('ev149SavedVariants');if(old)old.remove();renderEditorVariantTiles();});return result;};
  }
  const priorEdOpen=window.edOpen;
  if(typeof priorEdOpen==='function'){
    window.edOpen=function(){window.__ethvelleV150ActiveVariant=null;if(typeof ED!=='undefined')ED.previewVariant=null;const result=priorEdOpen.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(()=>{const old=$('ev149SavedVariants');if(old)old.remove();renderEditorVariantTiles();});return result;};
  }
  const priorEdSelect=window.edSelect;
  if(typeof priorEdSelect==='function'){
    window.edSelect=function(key){window.__ethvelleV150ActiveVariant=null;if(typeof ED!=='undefined')ED.previewVariant=null;return priorEdSelect.apply(this,arguments);};
  }

  /* Face/hand action panel: show only the custom tile's border when its override is selected. */
  function actionForCurrentClip(){
    try{const clip=typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&typeof getClipByRef==='function'?getClipByRef(selectedClip):null;return clip&&clip.faceActions||{};}catch(_e){return {};}
  }
  function syncExclusiveActionBorders(){
    const back=$('ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop');if(!back||!back.classList.contains('active'))return;
    const a=actionForCurrentClip();
    const suppress={
      'face expression':!!a.customExpressionId,
      'facial expression':!!a.customExpressionId,
      'eyes':!!a.customEyeId,'eye':!!a.customEyeId,
      'mouth':!!(a.customMouthOpenId||a.customMouthClosedId),
      'mouth movement':!!(a.customMouthOpenId||a.customMouthClosedId),
      'left hand':!!a.customGestureLId,'left hand gesture':!!a.customGestureLId,
      'right hand':!!a.customGestureRId,'right hand gesture':!!a.customGestureRId
    };
    back.querySelectorAll('.evpa-section').forEach(section=>{
      const heading=section.querySelector('.evpa-section-title');const label=String(heading&&heading.textContent||'').trim().toLowerCase();
      if(!suppress[label])return;
      section.querySelectorAll('.evpa-choice:not(.evpa-custom-variant-choice)').forEach(button=>{button.classList.remove('active');button.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');});
    });
  }
  const priorOpenActions=window.openClipCharacterActions;
  if(typeof priorOpenActions==='function')window.openClipCharacterActions=function(){const result=priorOpenActions.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(syncExclusiveActionBorders);return result;};

  /* Keep the visible character in sync immediately as well as retaining the clip action. */
  function applyBuiltinToCharacter(field,value){
    const char=activeChar();if(!char||!char.sheet)return;
    if(!char.expr||typeof char.expr!=='object')char.expr=typeof sheetExprFresh==='function'?sheetExprFresh():{};
    const e=char.expr;
    if(field==='expression'){e.emo=value==='none'?null:value;e.customExpressionId=null;e.customEyeId=null;}
    else if(field==='eyes'){e.eyeState=value==='auto'?'auto':value;e.customEyeId=null;}
    else if(field==='mouth'){e.mouth=value==='talk'?'open':'closed';e.customMouthOpenId=null;e.customMouthClosedId=null;}
    else if(field==='leftHand'){e.customGestureLId=null;e.fistL=value==='fist';e.gestL=value==='none'||value==='fist'?null:value;}
    else if(field==='rightHand'){e.customGestureRId=null;e.fistR=value==='fist';e.gestR=value==='none'||value==='fist'?null:value;}
    if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();
  }
  const priorUpdateFace=window.updateClipFaceAction;
  if(typeof priorUpdateFace==='function')window.updateClipFaceAction=function(field,value){const result=priorUpdateFace.apply(this,arguments);applyBuiltinToCharacter(field,value);if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();return result;};
  const priorUpdateCustom=window.updateClipCustomVariant;
  if(typeof priorUpdateCustom==='function')window.updateClipCustomVariant=function(field,id){const result=priorUpdateCustom.apply(this,arguments);const char=activeChar(),variants=char&&char.sheet&&char.sheet.customVariants||{};let item=null;['headParts','expressions','gestures'].some(cat=>{item=(Array.isArray(variants[cat])?variants[cat]:[]).find(v=>v&&v.id===id)||null;return !!item;});if(item)applyVariantToCharacter(item,field);requestAnimationFrame(syncExclusiveActionBorders);return result;};

  /* A few models may have been opened before this final patch ran. Normalize their hand slots. */
  if(typeof characters!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(characters))characters.forEach(removeAccidentalMirror);
  const back=$('ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop');if(back&&back.classList.contains('active'))updateHeldPanelUI(activeChar(),getSide(back));
})();
</script>

  #toolPanel .evlt-controls .evpa-range,

    display:block!important;margin:7px 0 8px!important;color:#D7DBF1!important;

  }
  #toolPanel .evlt-controls .evpa-range>span,
  #speedPanelBody .evpa-range>span{

  }

<script id="ethvelle-v152-model-speed-volume-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV152Installed)return;
  window.__ethvelleV152Installed=true;
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const renderCharacterNow=()=>{
    try{if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle model-part frame refresh:',err);}
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      try{if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();}catch(_e){}
      try{if(typeof window.drawStick==='function')window.drawStick();}catch(_e){}
    });
  };
  function getEditorCharacter(){
    try{if(typeof ED!=='undefined'&&ED&&ED.kind==='edit'&&Number.isInteger(ED.editIdx)&&Array.isArray(characters)&&characters[ED.editIdx])return characters[ED.editIdx];}catch(_e){}
    try{return typeof window.activeCharacter==='function'?window.activeCharacter():null;}catch(_e){return null;}
  }
  function ensureDefaultParts(char){
    if(!char)return null;
    if(!char.modelPartDefaults||typeof char.modelPartDefaults!=='object'){
      const saved=char.expr&&char.expr.__modelPartDefaults&&typeof char.expr.__modelPartDefaults==='object'?char.expr.__modelPartDefaults:{};
      char.modelPartDefaults={...saved};
    }
    return char.modelPartDefaults;
  }
  function setDefaultVariant(char,field,id){
    const d=ensureDefaultParts(char);if(!d)return;
    const value=String(id||'')||null;
    if(field==='customExpressionId'){
      d.customExpressionId=value;d.customEyeId=null;d.customMouthOpenId=null;d.customMouthClosedId=null;
    }else if(field==='customEyeId'){
      d.customEyeId=value;d.customExpressionId=null;
    }else if(field==='customMouthOpenId'){
      d.customMouthOpenId=value;d.customMouthClosedId=null;
    }else if(field==='customMouthClosedId'){
      d.customMouthClosedId=value;d.customMouthOpenId=null;
    }else if(field==='customGestureLId'||field==='customGestureRId')d[field]=value;
    if(!char.expr||typeof char.expr!=='object')char.expr=typeof window.sheetExprFresh==='function'?window.sheetExprFresh():{};
    char.expr[field]=value;
    if(field==='customMouthOpenId'&&value)char.expr.mouth='open';
    if(field==='customMouthClosedId'&&value)char.expr.mouth='closed';
    char.expr.__modelPartDefaults={...d};
  }
  function persistPartDefaults(char){
    try{if(char&&char.sheet&&typeof ED!=='undefined'&&ED&&ED.extraVariants)char.sheet.customVariants=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ED.extraVariants));}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof window.persistProjectState==='function')window.persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});}catch(_e){}
  }

  /* Model Lab custom tiles are character defaults, not temporary playhead-only actions. */
  document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
    const button=event.target&&event.target.closest?event.target.closest('#sheetEd #edLayers .ev150-variant-chip'):null;
    if(!button)return;
    const field=button.dataset.variantField,id=button.dataset.variantId;
    if(!field||!id)return;
    const char=getEditorCharacter();if(!char||!char.sheet)return;
    setDefaultVariant(char,field,id);
    persistPartDefaults(char);
    renderCharacterNow();
  },true);

  /* Restore Model Lab defaults after the timeline's frame reset. Clip-specific custom IDs win. */
  const oldReset=window.resetCharacterForTimelineFrame;
  if(typeof oldReset==='function'&&!oldReset.__ethvelleV152Defaults){
    const wrappedReset=function(char){
      const result=oldReset.apply(this,arguments);
      if(char&&char.sheet){
        if(!char.expr||typeof char.expr!=='object')char.expr=typeof window.sheetExprFresh==='function'?window.sheetExprFresh():{};
        const d=char.modelPartDefaults&&typeof char.modelPartDefaults==='object'?char.modelPartDefaults:(char.expr.__modelPartDefaults||{});
        char.modelPartDefaults={...d};
        ['customExpressionId','customEyeId','customMouthOpenId','customMouthClosedId','customGestureLId','customGestureRId'].forEach(k=>{char.expr[k]=d[k]||null;});
        if(d.customMouthOpenId)char.expr.mouth='open';else if(d.customMouthClosedId)char.expr.mouth='closed';
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrappedReset.__ethvelleV152Defaults=true;window.resetCharacterForTimelineFrame=wrappedReset;
  }
  const oldApplyPose=window.applyPoseToBones;
  if(typeof oldApplyPose==='function'&&!oldApplyPose.__ethvelleV152Defaults){
    const wrappedApplyPose=function(pose,targetBones,targetBaseAngles,targetHipRestY){
      const result=oldApplyPose.apply(this,arguments);
      let b=targetBones;
      if(b===undefined){try{b=typeof bones!=='undefined'?bones:null;}catch(_e){b=null;}}
      let char=null;try{if(Array.isArray(characters)&&b)char=characters.find(c=>c&&c.bones===b)||null;}catch(_e){}
      if(char&&char.sheet){
        if(!char.expr||typeof char.expr!=='object')char.expr=typeof window.sheetExprFresh==='function'?window.sheetExprFresh():{};
        const d=char.modelPartDefaults&&typeof char.modelPartDefaults==='object'?char.modelPartDefaults:(char.expr.__modelPartDefaults||{});
        char.modelPartDefaults={...d};
        const source=pose||{},modern=source.__ethvelleModernPose||(typeof window.ethvelleIs21Pose==='function'&&window.ethvelleIs21Pose(source)?source:null)||{};
        const pairs=[['customExpressionId','__customExpressionId'],['customEyeId','__customEyeId'],['customMouthOpenId','__customMouthOpenId'],['customMouthClosedId','__customMouthClosedId'],['customGestureLId','__customGestureLId'],['customGestureRId','__customGestureRId']];
        pairs.forEach(([field,meta])=>{char.expr[field]=modern[meta]||d[field]||null;});
        if(modern.__mouth==='open'||d.customMouthOpenId)char.expr.mouth='open';
        else if(modern.__mouth==='hold')char.expr.mouth='hold';
        else if(modern.__mouth==='closed'||d.customMouthClosedId)char.expr.mouth='closed';
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrappedApplyPose.__ethvelleV152Defaults=true;window.applyPoseToBones=wrappedApplyPose;
  }

  /* Explicit built-in Face & Hands choices clear the matching model-wide custom default. */
  const oldUpdateFace=window.updateClipFaceAction;
  if(typeof oldUpdateFace==='function'&&!oldUpdateFace.__ethvelleV152Defaults){
    const wrappedUpdateFace=function(field,value){
      const char=(()=>{try{return typeof window.activeCharacter==='function'?window.activeCharacter():null;}catch(_e){return null;}})();
      const d=char?ensureDefaultParts(char):null;
      if(d){
        if(field==='expression'){d.customExpressionId=null;}
        else if(field==='eyes'){d.customEyeId=null;}
        else if(field==='mouth'){d.customMouthOpenId=null;d.customMouthClosedId=null;}
        else if(field==='leftHand')d.customGestureLId=null;
        else if(field==='rightHand')d.customGestureRId=null;
        if(char&&char.expr)char.expr.__modelPartDefaults={...d};
      }
      const result=oldUpdateFace.apply(this,arguments);
      if(char)try{if(typeof window.persistProjectState==='function')window.persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});}catch(_e){}
      renderCharacterNow();
      return result;
    };
    wrappedUpdateFace.__ethvelleV152Defaults=true;window.updateClipFaceAction=wrappedUpdateFace;
  }

  /* Speed uses the same slider-card UI as Mask. Each speed stop has an equal visual gap. */
  const speedStops=[.25,.5,1,1.5,2,3];
  const speedStopIndex=value=>{const n=Number(value)||1;let best=0,dist=Infinity;speedStops.forEach((v,i)=>{const d=Math.abs(v-n);if(d<dist){dist=d;best=i;}});return best;};
  function paintSpeedPanel(){
    const slider=$('evvSpeedSlider'),clip=typeof window.speedTargetClip==='function'?window.speedTargetClip():null;
    if(!slider)return;
    const speed=Math.max(.25,Math.min(3,Number(clip&&clip.speed)||1)),index=speedStopIndex(speed),pct=index/(speedStops.length-1)*100;
    slider.value=String(index);slider.style.setProperty('--evv-progress',pct+'%');
    const output=$('speedVal');if(output)output.textContent=speed.toFixed(2)+'x';
  }
  window.speedPanelSetStop=function(index){
    const i=Math.max(0,Math.min(speedStops.length-1,Math.round(Number(index)||0)));
    if(typeof window.speedPanelSetSpeed==='function')window.speedPanelSetSpeed(speedStops[i]);
  };
  window.renderSpeedPanel=function(){
    const body=$('speedPanelBody');if(!body)return;
    const clip=typeof window.speedTargetClip==='function'?window.speedTargetClip():null;
    if(!clip){body.innerHTML='<p class="field-label">Select an animation, video, image, or audio clip on the timeline to change its speed.</p>';return;}
    const speed=Math.max(.25,Math.min(3,Number(clip.speed)||1)),index=speedStopIndex(speed);
    const name=typeof window.escapeHtml==='function'?window.escapeHtml(clip.name||(typeof window.isStickmanSpeedTarget==='function'&&window.isStickmanSpeedTarget(clip)?'Animation':'Clip')):String(clip.name||'Clip');
    const marks=speedStops.map((v,i)=>`<span style="left:${i/(speedStops.length-1)*100}%">${v}x</span>`).join('');
    const ticks=speedStops.map((v,i)=>`<i style="left:${i/(speedStops.length-1)*100}%"></i>`).join('');
    body.innerHTML=`<div class="evv-speed-panel">
      <div class="evv-speed-head"><span class="evv-speed-title">Playback speed</span></div>
      <p class="evv-speed-clip" id="speedPanelHeader">${name}</p>
      <div class="evv-slider-card evv-speed-slider-card">
        <div class="evv-slider-head"><label for="evvSpeedSlider">Speed</label><output id="speedVal">${speed.toFixed(2)}x</output></div>
        <input id="evvSpeedSlider" type="range" min="0" max="${speedStops.length-1}" step="1" value="${index}" style="--evv-progress:${index/(speedStops.length-1)*100}%" oninput="window.speedPanelSetStop(this.value)" aria-label="Playback speed">
        <div class="evv-speed-stop-ticks" aria-hidden="true">${ticks}</div>
        <div class="evv-speed-marks" aria-hidden="true">${marks}</div>
      </div>
      <button type="button" class="evv-speed-done" onclick="closePanel()">Done</button>
    </div>`;
    paintSpeedPanel();
  };
  const oldSetSpeed=window.speedPanelSetSpeed;
  if(typeof oldSetSpeed==='function'&&!oldSetSpeed.__ethvelleV153Stops){
    const wrappedSetSpeed=function(value){
      const result=oldSetSpeed.apply(this,arguments);
      const clip=typeof window.speedTargetClip==='function'?window.speedTargetClip():null;
      const header=$('speedPanelHeader');if(header&&clip)header.textContent=clip.name||(typeof window.isStickmanSpeedTarget==='function'&&window.isStickmanSpeedTarget(clip)?'Animation':'Clip');
      paintSpeedPanel();
      return result;
    };
    wrappedSetSpeed.__ethvelleV153Stops=true;window.speedPanelSetSpeed=wrappedSetSpeed;
  }

  /* Remove Extract Audio from Volume only; the editor's separate Extract Audio tool remains available. */
  const oldVolumeRender=window.renderVolumePanel;
  if(typeof oldVolumeRender==='function'&&!oldVolumeRender.__ethvelleV152NoExtract){
    const wrappedVolumeRender=function(){const result=oldVolumeRender.apply(this,arguments);const button=$('volumePanelBody')?.querySelector('.evv-volume-extract');if(button)button.remove();return result;};
    wrappedVolumeRender.__ethvelleV152NoExtract=true;window.renderVolumePanel=wrappedVolumeRender;
  }

  /* Repaint the active model immediately when a custom variant is applied through Face & Hands. */
  const oldUpdateCustom=window.updateClipCustomVariant;
  if(typeof oldUpdateCustom==='function'&&!oldUpdateCustom.__ethvelleV152Immediate){
    const wrappedUpdateCustom=function(){const result=oldUpdateCustom.apply(this,arguments);renderCharacterNow();return result;};
    wrappedUpdateCustom.__ethvelleV152Immediate=true;window.updateClipCustomVariant=wrappedUpdateCustom;
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v153-hand-transfer-runtime">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV153HandTransfer)return;
  window.__ethvelleV153HandTransfer=true;
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const getChar=()=>{try{return typeof window.activeCharacter==='function'?window.activeCharacter():null;}catch(_e){return null;}};
  function handSide(back){
    const row=back&&back.querySelector('.evpa-action-side');if(!row)return 'R';
    const buttons=Array.from(row.querySelectorAll('button'));
    const left=buttons.find(b=>/left/i.test(b.textContent||''));
    const right=buttons.find(b=>/right/i.test(b.textContent||''));
    if(left&&left.classList.contains('active'))return 'L';
    if(right&&right.classList.contains('active'))return 'R';
    return 'R';
  }
  function ensureProps(char){
    if(!char)return null;
    if(!char.handProps||typeof char.handProps!=='object')char.handProps={L:null,R:null};
    if(!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(char.handProps,'L'))char.handProps.L=null;
    if(!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(char.handProps,'R'))char.handProps.R=null;
    return char.handProps;
  }
  function moveObjectToHand(char,from,to){
    const props=ensureProps(char);if(!props||props[to]||!props[from]||!props[from].src)return false;
    props[to]=props[from];props[from]=null;
    const clip=(()=>{try{return typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&typeof getClipByRef==='function'?getClipByRef(selectedClip):null;}catch(_e){return null;}})();
    const parts=char.sheet&&char.sheet.parts||{};
    if(clip){
      const actions={...(clip.faceActions&&typeof clip.faceActions==='object'?clip.faceActions:{})};
      const oldField=from==='L'?'leftHand':'rightHand',newField=to==='L'?'leftHand':'rightHand';
      if(parts['hand'+from+'_hold'])actions[oldField]='none';
      if(parts['hand'+to+'_hold'])actions[newField]='hold';
      clip.faceActions=actions;
    }
    try{if(typeof window.drawStick==='function')window.drawStick();}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof window.persistProjectState==='function')window.persistProjectState();}catch(_e){}
    return true;
  }
  const baseSetSide=window.setCharacterPropSide;
  if(typeof baseSetSide==='function'&&!baseSetSide.__ethvelleV153Transfer){
    const wrapped=function(side){
      if(side!=='L'&&side!=='R')return;
      const back=$('ethvelleCharacterPropsBackdrop'),from=handSide(back),char=getChar();
      if(char&&side!==from)moveObjectToHand(char,from,side);
      const result=baseSetSide.apply(this,arguments);
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__ethvelleV153Transfer=true;window.setCharacterPropSide=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>

<style id="ethvelle-v154-speed-part-menu-css">
  /* Speed deliberately reuses the Volume panel's layout and visual language. */
  #speedPanelBody .evv-volume-panel{display:flex!important;flex-direction:column!important;gap:15px!important;padding:2px 0 6px!important;}

  #speedPanelBody .evv-volume-name{min-width:0!important;overflow:hidden!important;text-overflow:ellipsis!important;white-space:nowrap!important;color:#D9DCE8!important;font-size:12px!important;font-weight:700!important;}
  #speedPanelBody .evv-volume-value{flex:0 0 auto!important;color:#AFC4FF!important;font-size:13px!important;font-weight:800!important;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums!important;}
  #speedPanelBody .evv-volume-slider-wrap{position:relative!important;padding:7px 0 3px!important;}
  #speedPanelBody .evv-volume-slider{display:block!important;width:100%!important;height:22px!important;margin:0!important;padding:0!important;appearance:none!important;-webkit-appearance:none!important;background:transparent!important;outline:none!important;cursor:pointer!important;touch-action:pan-x!important;}
  #speedPanelBody .evv-volume-slider::-webkit-slider-runnable-track{height:4px!important;border-radius:99px!important;background:linear-gradient(to right,#3F6FFF 0%,#3F6FFF var(--evv-volume-fill,50%),#3A3A45 var(--evv-volume-fill,50%),#3A3A45 100%)!important;}
  #speedPanelBody .evv-volume-slider::-webkit-slider-thumb{appearance:none!important;-webkit-appearance:none!important;width:16px!important;height:16px!important;margin-top:-6px!important;border-radius:50%!important;background:#F5F7FF!important;border:3px solid #4776FF!important;box-shadow:0 0 0 2px rgba(71,118,255,.16)!important;}

<script id="ethvelle-v154-runtime">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV154Installed)return;
  window.__ethvelleV154Installed=true;
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const esc=value=>String(value==null?'':value).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
  const SPEED_STOPS=[0.25,0.5,1,1.5,2,3];
  const activeChar=()=>{try{return typeof window.activeCharacter==='function'?window.activeCharacter():null;}catch(_e){return null;}};
  const modelLabChar=()=>{try{if(typeof ED!=='undefined'&&ED&&ED.kind==='edit'&&Number.isInteger(ED.editIdx)&&typeof characters!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(characters)&&characters[ED.editIdx])return characters[ED.editIdx];}catch(_e){}return activeChar();};
  const cloneVariants=variants=>{try{return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(variants||{headParts:[],expressions:[],gestures:[]}));}catch(_e){return {headParts:[],expressions:[],gestures:[]};}};

  /* Uploaded artwork is stored under stable type labels, never the user's source filename. */
  function normalizeVariantNames(variants){
    if(!variants||typeof variants!=='object')return false;
    ['headParts','expressions','gestures'].forEach(k=>{if(!Array.isArray(variants[k]))variants[k]=[];});
    const head=[];
    variants.headParts.forEach((item,index)=>{if(item&&typeof item==='object')head.push({item,order:index,when:Number(item.createdAt)||0});});
    variants.expressions.forEach((item,index)=>{if(item&&typeof item==='object')head.push({item,order:variants.headParts.length+index,when:Number(item.createdAt)||0});});
    head.sort((a,b)=>(a.when&&b.when?a.when-b.when:a.when?-1:b.when?1:a.order-b.order));
    head.forEach((entry,index)=>{
      const label='Custom Head '+(index+1),item=entry.item;
      item.name=label;item.fileName=label;item.originalFileName=label;item.displayName=label;
    });
    const hands=variants.gestures.map((item,index)=>({item,index,when:Number(item&&item.createdAt)||0})).filter(x=>x.item&&typeof x.item==='object');
    hands.sort((a,b)=>(a.when&&b.when?a.when-b.when:a.when?-1:b.when?1:a.index-b.index));
    hands.forEach((entry,index)=>{
      const label='Custom Hand '+(index+1),item=entry.item;
      item.name=label;item.fileName=label;item.originalFileName=label;item.displayName=label;
    });
    return true;
  }
  function normalizeCharacter(char){
    if(!char||!char.sheet)return;
    if(!char.sheet.customVariants||typeof char.sheet.customVariants!=='object')char.sheet.customVariants={headParts:[],expressions:[],gestures:[]};
    normalizeVariantNames(char.sheet.customVariants);
  }
  function normalizeEditor(){try{if(typeof ED!=='undefined'&&ED&&ED.extraVariants)normalizeVariantNames(ED.extraVariants);}catch(_e){}}
  const oldAssetUpdate=window.updateModelAssetFromChar;
  if(typeof oldAssetUpdate==='function'&&!oldAssetUpdate.__ethvelleV154Names){
    const wrapped=function(char){normalizeCharacter(char);normalizeEditor();return oldAssetUpdate.apply(this,arguments);};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV154Names=true;window.updateModelAssetFromChar=wrapped;
  }
  const oldEditorSync=window.edSyncUI;
  if(typeof oldEditorSync==='function'&&!oldEditorSync.__ethvelleV154Names){

    wrapped.__ethvelleV154Names=true;window.edSyncUI=wrapped;
  }

  }
  const oldCompile=window.edCompile;
  if(typeof oldCompile==='function'&&!oldCompile.__ethvelleV154Names){
    const wrapped=function(){normalizeEditor();return oldCompile.apply(this,arguments);};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV154Names=true;window.edCompile=wrapped;
  }
  normalizeCharacter(activeChar());normalizeEditor();
  const oldOpenCharacterActions=window.openClipCharacterActions;
  if(typeof oldOpenCharacterActions==='function'&&!oldOpenCharacterActions.__ethvelleV154Names){
    const wrapped=function(){normalizeCharacter(activeChar());return oldOpenCharacterActions.apply(this,arguments);};

  }

  /* Speed panel matches the Volume panel. The six labels are evenly spaced reference points,
     while the control itself remains continuous between every point. */
  const speedIndex=value=>{const n=Number(value)||1;let best=0,dist=Infinity;SPEED_STOPS.forEach((v,i)=>{const d=Math.abs(v-n);if(d<dist){dist=d;best=i;}});return best;};
  function speedPosition(value){
    const n=Math.max(SPEED_STOPS[0],Math.min(SPEED_STOPS[SPEED_STOPS.length-1],Number(value)||1));
    for(let i=0;i<SPEED_STOPS.length-1;i++){
      const a=SPEED_STOPS[i],b=SPEED_STOPS[i+1];
      if(n<=b)return i+(n-a)/(b-a);
    }
    return SPEED_STOPS.length-1;
  }
  function speedValueAtPosition(position){
    const p=Math.max(0,Math.min(SPEED_STOPS.length-1,Number(position)||0));
    const i=Math.min(SPEED_STOPS.length-2,Math.floor(p));
    const f=p-i;
    return SPEED_STOPS[i]+(SPEED_STOPS[i+1]-SPEED_STOPS[i])*f;
  }
  const speedText=value=>{const v=Number(value);return (Number.isFinite(v)?String(Number(v.toFixed(2))):'1')+'x';};
  function paintSpeedPanel(){
    const slider=$('evvSpeedSlider');if(!slider)return;
    const clip=typeof window.speedTargetClip==='function'?window.speedTargetClip():null;
    const speed=Math.max(.25,Math.min(3,Number(clip&&clip.speed)||1));
    const pos=speedPosition(speed),pct=pos/(SPEED_STOPS.length-1)*100,nearest=speedIndex(speed);
    slider.value=pos.toFixed(2);slider.style.setProperty('--evv-volume-fill',pct+'%');
    const output=$('speedVal');if(output)output.textContent=speedText(speed);
    const header=$('speedPanelHeader');if(header)header.textContent=clip?(clip.name||(typeof window.isStickmanSpeedTarget==='function'&&window.isStickmanSpeedTarget(clip)?'Animation':'Clip')):'';
    const marks=$('speedPanelMarks');if(marks)marks.querySelectorAll('span').forEach((node,i)=>{node.style.color=i===nearest?'#FFFFFF':'#8B91A2';});
  }
  window.speedPanelSetStop=function(position){
    const speed=speedValueAtPosition(position);
    if(typeof window.speedPanelSetSpeed==='function')window.speedPanelSetSpeed(speed);
  };
  const originalSpeedSetter=window.speedPanelSetSpeed;
  if(typeof originalSpeedSetter==='function'&&!originalSpeedSetter.__ethvelleV155Continuous){
    const wrapped=function(value){const result=originalSpeedSetter.apply(this,arguments);paintSpeedPanel();return result;};
    wrapped.__ethvelleV155Continuous=true;window.speedPanelSetSpeed=wrapped;
  }
  window.renderSpeedPanel=function(){
    const body=$('speedPanelBody');if(!body)return;
    const clip=typeof window.speedTargetClip==='function'?window.speedTargetClip():null;
    if(!clip){body.innerHTML='<p class="field-label">Select an animation, video, image, or audio clip on the timeline to change its speed.</p>';return;}
    const speed=Math.max(.25,Math.min(3,Number(clip.speed)||1)),pos=speedPosition(speed);
    const name=esc(clip.name||(typeof window.isStickmanSpeedTarget==='function'&&window.isStickmanSpeedTarget(clip)?'Animation':'Clip'));
    const marks=SPEED_STOPS.map((v,i)=>'<span data-stop="'+i+'">'+speedText(v)+'</span>').join('');
    body.innerHTML='<div class="evv-volume-panel evv-speed-volume-panel">'+
      '<div class="evv-volume-head"><span class="evv-volume-name" id="speedPanelHeader">'+name+'</span><output class="evv-volume-value" id="speedVal">'+speedText(speed)+'</output></div>'+
      '<div class="evv-volume-slider-wrap"><input id="evvSpeedSlider" class="evv-volume-slider" type="range" min="0" max="'+(SPEED_STOPS.length-1)+'" step="0.01" value="'+pos.toFixed(2)+'" style="--evv-volume-fill:'+(pos/(SPEED_STOPS.length-1)*100)+'%" oninput="window.speedPanelSetStop(this.value)" aria-label="Playback speed"></div>'+
      '<div class="evv-volume-marks" id="speedPanelMarks">'+marks+'</div>'+
      '<button type="button" class="evv-volume-done" onclick="closePanel()">Done</button>'+
    '</div>';
    paintSpeedPanel();
  };

  /* Long-press menu targets body-part tiles in both Model Editing and Character Edit. */
  let partMenuTarget=null,longPressState=null,longPressTimer=0,ignoreClickTarget=null,ignoreClickUntil=0;
  let replaceTarget=null;
  let partMenuAnchor=null;
  function ensureMenu(){
    let back=$('evvPartActionBackdrop');if(back)return back;
    back=document.createElement('div');back.id='evvPartActionBackdrop';
    back.innerHTML='<div class="evv-part-action-sheet" role="dialog" aria-modal="false" aria-label="Body part options"><p class="evv-part-action-title">Part options</p><p class="evv-part-action-subtitle" id="evvPartActionSubtitle"></p><div class="evv-part-action-buttons"><button type="button" data-action="edit">Edit</button><button type="button" data-action="close">Close</button><button type="button" data-action="delete">Delete</button></div></div>';
    (document.getElementById('screen-editor')||document.body).appendChild(back);
    back.querySelectorAll('[data-action]').forEach(button=>button.addEventListener('click',()=>runMenuAction(button.dataset.action)));
    document.addEventListener('pointerdown',event=>{
      const root=$('evvPartActionBackdrop');if(!root||!root.classList.contains('active'))return;
      const sheet=root.querySelector('.evv-part-action-sheet');
      if((sheet&&sheet.contains(event.target))||(partMenuAnchor&&partMenuAnchor.contains(event.target)))return;
      closeMenu();
    },true);
    return back;
  }
  function closeMenu(){const back=$('evvPartActionBackdrop');if(back)back.classList.remove('active');partMenuTarget=null;partMenuAnchor=null;}
  function categoriesOf(char){
    const variants=char&&char.sheet&&char.sheet.customVariants;
    return variants&&typeof variants==='object'?variants:{headParts:[],expressions:[],gestures:[]};
  }
  function fieldForItem(category,item){
    if(category==='expressions')return 'customExpressionId';
    if(category==='headParts'){
      if(item.kind==='custom-eyes')return 'customEyeId';
      if(item.kind==='mouth-closed')return 'customMouthClosedId';
      return item.kind==='mouth-open'?'customMouthOpenId':'customEyeId';
    }
    if(category==='gestures')return item.side==='L'||item.kind==='left-hand-gesture'?'customGestureLId':'customGestureRId';
    return null;
  }
  function findVariant(variants,category,id){const list=variants&&Array.isArray(variants[category])?variants[category]:[];return list.find(item=>item&&String(item.id)===String(id))||null;}
  function findItemByTile(char,category,tile){
    const img=tile.querySelector('img'),src=img&&(img.getAttribute('src')||img.src),title=tile.title||'';
    const list=categoriesOf(char)[category]||[];
    return list.find(item=>item&&((src&&item.src===src)||(title&&item.name===title)))||null;
  }
  function actionRoute(section){
    const label=String(section&&section.querySelector('.evpa-section-title')?.textContent||'').trim().toLowerCase();
    if(label.includes('face expression'))return {category:'expressions',field:'customExpressionId',baseField:'expression',neutral:'none'};
    if(label==='eyes'||label==='eye')return {category:'headParts',field:'customEyeId',baseField:'eyes',neutral:'auto'};
    if(label==='mouth'||label.includes('mouth movement'))return {category:'headParts',field:'customMouthOpenId',baseField:'mouth',neutral:'preset'};
    if(label.includes('left hand'))return {category:'gestures',field:'customGestureLId',baseField:'leftHand',neutral:'none'};
    if(label.includes('right hand'))return {category:'gestures',field:'customGestureRId',baseField:'rightHand',neutral:'none'};
    if(label.includes('blink'))return {category:null,field:null,baseField:'blink',neutral:'auto'};
    return null;
  }
  function buildTarget(tile){
    if(tile.matches('#sheetEd #edLayers .ev150-variant-chip')){
      const id=tile.dataset.variantId,category=['headParts','expressions','gestures'].find(k=>findVariant((typeof ED!=='undefined'&&ED.extraVariants)||{},k,id));
      const variants=typeof ED!=='undefined'&&ED&&ED.extraVariants?ED.extraVariants:null,item=category&&findVariant(variants,category,id);
      return item?{kind:'variant',location:'model',category,itemId:id,field:fieldForItem(category,item),title:item.name||'Custom part',char:modelLabChar()}:null;
    }
    if(tile.matches('#sheetEd #edLayers .ed-chip[data-k]')&&!tile.classList.contains('ed-chip-custom')){
      return {kind:'base-part',location:'model',partKey:tile.dataset.k,title:(tile.querySelector('.n')?.textContent||tile.dataset.k),char:modelLabChar()};
    }
    if(tile.matches('#ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop .evpa-custom-variant-choice')){
      const section=tile.closest('.evpa-section'),route=actionRoute(section),char=activeChar();if(!route||!char)return null;
      const category=route.category;if(!category)return null;
      const item=findItemByTile(char,category,tile);if(!item)return null;
      return {kind:'variant',location:'character',category,itemId:item.id,field:fieldForItem(category,item)||route.field,title:item.name||'Custom part',char};
    }
    if(tile.matches('#ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop .evpa-choice:not(.evpa-choice-add)')){
      const section=tile.closest('.evpa-section'),route=actionRoute(section);if(!route)return null;
      const handler=tile.getAttribute('onclick')||'',match=handler.match(/updateClipFaceAction\(['"]([^'"]+)['"]\s*,\s*['"]([^'"]+)['"]\)/);
      return {kind:'action-part',location:'character',field:route.field,baseField:match?match[1]:route.baseField,neutral:route.neutral,title:tile.querySelector('.evpa-choice-label')?.textContent||route.baseField,char:activeChar()};
    }
    return null;
  }
  function openMenu(tile){
    const target=buildTarget(tile);if(!target)return;
    partMenuTarget=target;partMenuAnchor=tile;
    const back=ensureMenu(),sheet=back.querySelector('.evv-part-action-sheet'),subtitle=$('evvPartActionSubtitle');
    if(subtitle)subtitle.textContent=target.title||'Body part';
    back.classList.add('active');
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      if(!sheet||!tile.isConnected)return;
      const rect=tile.getBoundingClientRect(),width=sheet.offsetWidth||236,height=sheet.offsetHeight||116,gap=8,pad=8;
      let left=rect.right+gap;
      if(left+width>window.innerWidth-pad)left=rect.left-width-gap;
      left=Math.max(pad,Math.min(window.innerWidth-width-pad,left));
      let top=rect.top+(rect.height-height)/2;
      top=Math.max(pad,Math.min(window.innerHeight-height-pad,top));
      sheet.style.left=Math.round(left)+'px';sheet.style.top=Math.round(top)+'px';
    });
  }
  function cancelLongPress(){if(longPressTimer){clearTimeout(longPressTimer);longPressTimer=0;}longPressState=null;}
  function longPressTile(node){
    return node&&node.closest?node.closest('#sheetEd #edLayers .ed-chip[data-k],#sheetEd #edLayers .ev150-variant-chip,#ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop .evpa-choice:not(.evpa-choice-add)'):null;
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',event=>{
    if(event.button!==undefined&&event.button!==0)return;
    const tile=longPressTile(event.target);if(!tile)return;
    cancelLongPress();longPressState={target:tile,id:event.pointerId,x:event.clientX,y:event.clientY,fired:false};tile.classList.add('evv-longpress-target');
    longPressTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
      longPressTimer=0;if(!longPressState||!longPressState.target.isConnected)return;
      longPressState.fired=true;ignoreClickTarget=tile;ignoreClickUntil=Date.now()+1200;
      try{navigator.vibrate&&navigator.vibrate(20);}catch(_e){}
      openMenu(tile);
    },540);
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('pointermove',event=>{
    if(!longPressState||event.pointerId!==longPressState.id)return;
    if(Math.hypot(event.clientX-longPressState.x,event.clientY-longPressState.y)>11)cancelLongPress();
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',event=>{if(longPressState&&event.pointerId===longPressState.id){const node=longPressState.target;if(node)node.classList.remove('evv-longpress-target');const fired=longPressState.fired;cancelLongPress();if(fired)ignoreClickUntil=Date.now()+900;}},true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',cancelLongPress,true);
  document.addEventListener('contextmenu',event=>{if(longPressTile(event.target))event.preventDefault();},true);
  document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
    if(!ignoreClickTarget||Date.now()>ignoreClickUntil)return;
    if(event.target===ignoreClickTarget||ignoreClickTarget.contains(event.target)){
      event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();if(event.stopImmediatePropagation)event.stopImmediatePropagation();ignoreClickTarget=null;ignoreClickUntil=0;
    }
  },true);

  function persistChar(char){
    normalizeCharacter(char);
    try{if(typeof window.updateModelAssetFromChar==='function')window.updateModelAssetFromChar(char);}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof window.persistProjectState==='function')window.persistProjectState({skipHistory:true});}catch(_e){}
  }
  function refreshCharacter(){
    try{if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof window.drawStick==='function')window.drawStick();}catch(_e){}
  }
  function refreshModelLab(){
    try{if(typeof ED!=='undefined'&&ED&&ED.open){normalizeEditor();if(typeof window.edSyncUI==='function')window.edSyncUI();if(typeof window.edDraw==='function')window.edDraw();}}catch(_e){}
  }
  function refreshActionPanel(){
    const back=$('ethvelleClipFaceActionsBackdrop');if(!back||!back.classList.contains('active'))return;
    try{if(typeof window.closeClipCharacterActions==='function')window.closeClipCharacterActions();}catch(_e){}
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{if(typeof window.openClipCharacterActions==='function')window.openClipCharacterActions();}catch(_e){}});
  }
  function clearVariantReferences(char,id,field){
    const fields=['customExpressionId','customEyeId','customMouthOpenId','customMouthClosedId','customGestureLId','customGestureRId'];
    [char&&char.expr,char&&char.modelPartDefaults,char&&char.expr&&char.expr.__modelPartDefaults].forEach(obj=>{
      if(!obj)return;fields.forEach(key=>{if(String(obj[key]||'')===String(id))obj[key]=null;});
    });
    try{const clip=typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&typeof getClipByRef==='function'?getClipByRef(selectedClip):null;if(clip&&clip.faceActions){fields.forEach(key=>{if(String(clip.faceActions[key]||'')===String(id))clip.faceActions[key]=null;});}}catch(_e){}
    if(char&&char.expr)char.expr.__modelPartDefaults={...(char.modelPartDefaults||char.expr.__modelPartDefaults||{})};
  }
  function deleteVariant(target){
    const char=target.char||activeChar();if(!char)return;
    const variants=categoriesOf(char),list=variants[target.category];if(!Array.isArray(list))return;
    const item=list.find(v=>String(v&&v.id)===String(target.itemId));if(!item)return;
    clearVariantReferences(char,target.itemId,target.field);
    variants[target.category]=list.filter(v=>String(v&&v.id)!==String(target.itemId));
    char.sheet.customVariants=variants;
    try{if(typeof ED!=='undefined'&&ED&&ED.extraVariants){ED.extraVariants[target.category]=(ED.extraVariants[target.category]||[]).filter(v=>String(v&&v.id)!==String(target.itemId));normalizeEditor();}}catch(_e){}
    persistChar(char);refreshCharacter();refreshModelLab();refreshActionPanel();
    if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Custom part deleted',{icon:window.toastIcons&&window.toastIcons.check});
  }
  function deleteBasePart(target){
    try{
      if(typeof ED==='undefined'||!ED||!ED.open||!target.partKey)return;
      const key=target.partKey,art=typeof edArt==='function'?edArt(key):null;if(!art||!ED.arts||!ED.assign)return;
      if(typeof edPush==='function')edPush();
      const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=Math.max(1,Number(art.w)||1);canvas.height=Math.max(1,Number(art.h)||1);
      const blank={...art,canvas,src:canvas.toDataURL('image/png'),thumb:null,alpha:new Uint8Array(canvas.width*canvas.height)};
      const blankKey='evv_deleted_'+String(key)+'_'+Date.now().toString(36);ED.arts[blankKey]=blank;ED.assign[key]=blankKey;ED.sel=key;
      if(typeof edSyncUI==='function')edSyncUI();if(typeof edDraw==='function')edDraw();
      if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Body part removed from the model',{icon:window.toastIcons&&window.toastIcons.check});
    }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle part delete failed:',err);if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Could not remove this part',{icon:window.toastIcons&&window.toastIcons.info});}
  }
  function deleteActionPart(target){
    if(!target.baseField)return;
    try{if(typeof window.updateClipFaceAction==='function')window.updateClipFaceAction(target.baseField,target.neutral||'none');}catch(_e){}
    refreshCharacter();refreshActionPanel();
  }
  function getReplaceInput(){
    let input=$('evvReplaceCustomPartFile');if(input)return input;
    input=document.createElement('input');input.type='file';input.id='evvReplaceCustomPartFile';input.accept='image/png,image/webp,image/jpeg,image/*';input.hidden=true;
    input.addEventListener('change',()=>replaceVariantFromFile(input));(document.getElementById('screen-editor')||document.body).appendChild(input);return input;
  }
  function editVariant(target){replaceTarget=target;const input=getReplaceInput();input.value='';input.click();}
  function replaceVariantFromFile(input){
    const file=input.files&&input.files[0],target=replaceTarget;input.value='';replaceTarget=null;if(!file||!target)return;
    if(file.type&&!/^image\/(png|jpeg|webp|gif|avif)/i.test(file.type)){if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Choose a PNG, JPG or WebP image',{icon:window.toastIcons&&window.toastIcons.info});return;}
    const reader=new FileReader();reader.onerror=()=>{if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Could not read this image',{icon:window.toastIcons&&window.toastIcons.info});};
    reader.onload=()=>{const img=new Image();img.onerror=()=>{if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Could not open this image',{icon:window.toastIcons&&window.toastIcons.info});};img.onload=()=>{
      try{
        const max=1024,ratio=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(img.naturalWidth,img.naturalHeight)),w=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalWidth*ratio)),h=Math.max(1,Math.round(img.naturalHeight*ratio));
        const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=w;canvas.height=h;const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});if(!ctx)throw new Error('Canvas unavailable');ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,w,h);
        if(typeof window.edVariantRemoveFlatBackground==='function')window.edVariantRemoveFlatBackground(canvas);
        let item=null,char=target.char||activeChar();
        if(target.location==='model'&&typeof ED!=='undefined'&&ED&&ED.extraVariants)item=findVariant(ED.extraVariants,target.category,target.itemId);
        if(!item&&char)item=findVariant(categoriesOf(char),target.category,target.itemId);
        if(!item)throw new Error('Part no longer exists');
        item.src=canvas.toDataURL('image/png');item.w=w;item.h=h;item.updatedAt=Date.now();
        normalizeCharacter(char);
        if(typeof ED!=='undefined'&&ED&&ED.extraVariants){const edItem=findVariant(ED.extraVariants,target.category,target.itemId);if(edItem){edItem.src=item.src;edItem.w=w;edItem.h=h;normalizeEditor();}}
        if(char&&char.sheet)char.sheet.customVariants=cloneVariants(ED&&ED.extraVariants&&target.location==='model'?ED.extraVariants:categoriesOf(char));
        persistChar(char);refreshCharacter();refreshModelLab();refreshActionPanel();
        if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Custom part updated',{icon:window.toastIcons&&window.toastIcons.check});
      }catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle custom-part replacement failed:',err);if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Could not update this custom part',{icon:window.toastIcons&&window.toastIcons.info});}
    };img.src=String(reader.result||'');};reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  }
  function editBasePart(target){
    try{if(typeof window.edSelect==='function')window.edSelect(target.partKey);else if(typeof ED!=='undefined'&&ED){ED.sel=target.partKey;if(typeof edSyncUI==='function')edSyncUI();if(typeof edDraw==='function')edDraw();}}
    catch(_e){}
  }
  function editActionPart(){
    try{if(typeof window.openSelectedCharacterModelEditor==='function')window.openSelectedCharacterModelEditor();else if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Open Model Editing to edit this body part',{icon:window.toastIcons&&window.toastIcons.info});}
    catch(_e){}
  }
  function runMenuAction(action){
    const target=partMenuTarget;if(action==='close'||!target){closeMenu();return;}closeMenu();
    if(action==='edit'){
      if(target.kind==='variant')editVariant(target);
      else if(target.kind==='base-part')editBasePart(target);
      else if(target.kind==='action-part')editActionPart();
    }else if(action==='delete'){
      if(target.kind==='variant')deleteVariant(target);
      else if(target.kind==='base-part')deleteBasePart(target);
      else if(target.kind==='action-part')deleteActionPart(target);
    }
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v155-runtime">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV155Installed)return;window.__ethvelleV155Installed=true;
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const toast=(message,kind)=>{try{if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast(message,{icon:kind==='success'&&window.toastIcons?window.toastIcons.check:kind==='info'&&window.toastIcons?window.toastIcons.info:undefined,duration:3600});else console.warn('Ethvelle:',message);}catch(_e){console.warn('Ethvelle:',message);}};

  /* Retry the original durable media store after transient IndexedDB failures. If it cannot
     persist a file, return null so the import handlers can add an in-session object URL. */
  const previousMediaStore=window.__ethvelleStoreMediaFile;
  if(typeof previousMediaStore==='function'&&!previousMediaStore.__ethvelleV155Safe){
    const safeStore=async(file,handle)=>{
      try{
        const key=await previousMediaStore(file,handle||null);
        if(key) return key;
        throw new Error('Browser device storage did not return a media key.');
      }catch(err){
        console.warn('Ethvelle media storage unavailable; continuing with import fallback:',err);
        throw err; // Import handlers convert small files to project data and keep large files session-only.
      }
    };
    safeStore.__ethvelleV155Safe=true;window.__ethvelleStoreMediaFile=safeStore;
  }

  function safelyRefreshTimeline(){
    try{if(typeof window.renderOverlayLayers==='function')window.renderOverlayLayers();}catch(err){console.error('Ethvelle layer render retry:',err);}
    try{if(typeof window.renderBackgroundTrack==='function')window.renderBackgroundTrack();}catch(err){console.error('Ethvelle background render retry:',err);}
    try{if(typeof window.syncTimelineInnerWidth==='function')window.syncTimelineInnerWidth();}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof window.renderRuler==='function')window.renderRuler();}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof window.renderOverlayListPanel==='function')window.renderOverlayListPanel();}catch(_e){}
    try{if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();}catch(_e){}
  }
  function safePersist(){
    try{if(typeof window.persistProjectState==='function')window.persistProjectState();return true;}
    catch(err){console.error('Ethvelle project save after layer creation:',err);toast('The layer was added, but its project save failed. Export a backup before leaving.','info');return false;}
  }
  function getTimelineList(kind){
    try{
      if(kind==='background') return (typeof backgrounds!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(backgrounds))?backgrounds:[];
      return (typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays:[];
    }catch(_e){return [];}
  }
  function currentTimelineTime(){
    try{return Math.max(0,Number(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0);}catch(_e){return 0;}
  }
  function extendTimeline(end){
    const next=Math.max(0,Number(end)||0);
    try{if(typeof timelineDuration!=='undefined')timelineDuration=Math.max(Number(timelineDuration)||0,next);}catch(_e){}
    try{window.timelineDuration=Math.max(Number(window.timelineDuration)||0,next);}catch(_e){}
  }
  function wrapCreation(name,kind){
    const original=window[name];if(typeof original!=='function'||original.__ethvelleV155Guard)return;
    const wrapped=function(){
      const listBefore=getTimelineList(kind);
      const before=new Set(listBefore.map(o=>String(o&&o.id||'')));
      const opts=arguments[0]&&typeof arguments[0]==='object'?arguments[0]:null;
      try{return original.apply(this,arguments);}
      catch(err){
        console.error('Ethvelle '+kind+' creation failed:',err);
        const list=getTimelineList(kind);
        let created=list.find(o=>o&&!before.has(String(o.id||'')));
        const start=currentTimelineTime();
        if(!created&&kind==='shape'){
          const id='ov'+Date.now().toString(36)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,6);
          const palette=(typeof OVERLAY_COLORS!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(OVERLAY_COLORS))?OVERLAY_COLORS:['#F5A623','#FF6B9D','#7C9CFF'];
          created={id,name:'Shape '+(list.length+1),color:palette[list.length%palette.length],type:'shape',trimStart:start,trimEnd:start+2};
          list.push(created);extendTimeline(created.trimEnd);
        }
        if(!created&&kind==='media'&&opts&&opts.type&&opts.src){
          const id='ov'+Date.now().toString(36)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,6),len=Math.max(.12,Number(opts.duration)||3);
          created={id,name:opts.name||({video:'Video',audio:'Audio',image:'Image'}[opts.type]||'Layer'),color:'#7C9CFF',type:opts.type,src:opts.src,deviceMediaKey:opts.deviceMediaKey||undefined,trimStart:start,trimEnd:start+len,sourceDuration:Number(opts.sourceDuration)||len,thumbs:opts.thumbs||[],peaks:opts.peaks||[],speed:1,volume:opts.type==='video'||opts.type==='audio'?1:undefined,transform:opts.type==='image'||opts.type==='video'?{scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0}:null};
          list.push(created);extendTimeline(created.trimEnd);
        }
        if(!created&&kind==='background'){
          const src=typeof arguments[0]==='string'?arguments[0]:(opts&&opts.src);
          if(src){
            const len=Math.max(.12,Number(typeof BG_DEFAULT_DURATION!=='undefined'?BG_DEFAULT_DURATION:3)||3);
            created={id:'bg'+Date.now().toString(36),src,trimStart:start,trimEnd:start+len,transform:{scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0}};
            list.push(created);extendTimeline(created.trimEnd);
          }
        }
        if(created){
          safelyRefreshTimeline();safePersist();
          try{if(typeof window.onClipSelected==='function')window.onClipSelected(created,'add');}catch(_e){}
          try{if(kind==='shape'||kind==='media'){if(typeof window.selectClipForChrome==='function')window.selectClipForChrome(created.id);}else if(typeof window.refreshCurrentBgFromPlayhead==='function')window.refreshCurrentBgFromPlayhead();}catch(_e){}
          toast(kind==='shape'?'Shape layer added.':kind==='background'?'Background added.':'Media clip added.','success');
          return created;
        }
        toast('Could not add this '+kind+'. '+String(err&&err.message||'Please try again.'),'info');
        return null;
      }
    };
    wrapped.__ethvelleV155Guard=true;window[name]=wrapped;
  }
  wrapCreation('addOverlayLayer','shape');
  wrapCreation('addMediaOverlay','media');
  wrapCreation('addBackgroundClip','background');

  /* Legal-screen router is intentionally local to the Profile destinations. */
  window.openEthvelleLegalPage=function(which){
    const key=which==='terms'?'terms':'privacy';
    document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('active'));
    const page=$('screen-'+key);if(page)page.classList.add('active');
    try{if(typeof window.trackEvent==='function')window.trackEvent('screen_view',{screen:key});}catch(_e){}
  };
})();
</script>
