<script id="ethvelle-v164-mask-and-stop-at-end-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV164MaskAndStopInstalled)return;
  window.__ethvelleV164MaskAndStopInstalled=true;

  function visualClipById(id){
    const list=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays:(Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:[]);
    return list.find(item=>item&&String(item.id)===String(id)&&item.type!=='audio')||null;
  }
  function applyStandIn(el,clip,time,reference){
    if(!el||!clip||typeof window.ethvelleApplyOverlayVisualStyle!=='function')return;
    el.dataset.evMaskOwnerId=String(clip.id);
    if(time!=null)el.dataset.evMaskOwnerTime=String(time);
    const styleTime=Number.isFinite(Number(time))?Number(time):(Number(window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0);
    window.ethvelleApplyOverlayVisualStyle(el,clip,styleTime,reference||el,false);
  }
  function syncAllVisualStandIns(time){
    const frameTime=Number.isFinite(Number(time))?Number(time):(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec):Number(window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0);
    const list=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays:(Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:[]);
    const liveMap=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls:(window.overlayMediaEls||{});
    const chromaMap=(typeof overlayChromaCanvases!=='undefined'&&overlayChromaCanvases)?overlayChromaCanvases:(window.overlayChromaCanvases||{});
    const held=(window.renderOverlayMedia&&window.renderOverlayMedia.__evHeldOutgoing)||{};
    const layer=document.getElementById('overlayMediaLayer');
    if(layer){
      // Only a handful of stand-ins can exist (one per held/scrubbed video). The live
      // elements are styled by the compositor itself, so skip them here and avoid scanning
      // every project overlay on every rAF frame.
      layer.querySelectorAll('[data-ev-mask-owner-id]').forEach(el=>{
        const id=String(el.dataset.evMaskOwnerId||'');
        if(!id||liveMap[id]===el)return;
        const clip=visualClipById(id);if(!clip)return;
        const ownerTime=el.dataset.evMaskOwnerTime!=null?Number(el.dataset.evMaskOwnerTime):frameTime;
        const reference=liveMap[id];
        applyStandIn(el,clip,ownerTime,reference&&reference.isConnected?reference:el);
      });
      // A scrub hold can be detached temporarily while the video is adopted; keep it
      // covered even when it is not a current child of the layer.
      Object.keys(liveMap).forEach(id=>{
        const live=liveMap[id],hold=live&&live.__evScrubHoldCanvas;
        if(!hold||!hold.isConnected)return;
        const clip=visualClipById(id);if(clip)applyStandIn(hold,clip,frameTime,live);
      });
    }

    const bg=typeof currentBackgroundClip==='function'?currentBackgroundClip(frameTime):(typeof window.currentBackgroundClip==='function'?window.currentBackgroundClip(frameTime):null);
    const bgImg=document.getElementById('bgImgLayer');
    if(bg&&bgImg&&typeof window.ethvelleApplyOverlayVisualStyle==='function'){
      window.ethvelleApplyOverlayVisualStyle(bgImg,bg,frameTime,bgImg,true);
    }
  }

  const baseRender=window.renderOverlayMedia;
  if(typeof baseRender==='function'&&!baseRender.__ethvelleV164MaskPass){
    const wrapped=function(t){
      const frameTime=Number.isFinite(Number(t))?Number(t):(Number(window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0);
      const result=baseRender.apply(this,arguments);
      try{syncAllVisualStandIns(frameTime);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle mask visual sync:',err);}
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__ethvelleV164MaskPass=true;
    // Preserve the outgoing-frame registry used by the compositor and scrub-hold lifecycle.
    wrapped.__evHeldOutgoing=baseRender.__evHeldOutgoing||{};
    window.renderOverlayMedia=wrapped;
  }

  const baseBgTransform=window.applyBgTransform;
  if(typeof baseBgTransform==='function'&&!baseBgTransform.__ethvelleV164MaskPass){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const result=baseBgTransform.apply(this,arguments);
      try{syncAllVisualStandIns(Number(window.currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0);}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle background mask sync:',err);}
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__ethvelleV164MaskPass=true;
    window.applyBgTransform=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>
<script id="ethvelle-v165-playback-and-audio-fix-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvelleV165PlaybackAudioInstalled)return;
  window.__ethvelleV165PlaybackAudioInstalled=true;

  const BUDGET=window.__evDecoderBudget||{pool:3,warm:2};
  const POOL_MAX=BUDGET.pool,POOL_AHEAD_SECONDS=20,POOL_WARM_TARGET=BUDGET.warm;
  const pool=window.__ethvelleOverlayVideoPool||(window.__ethvelleOverlayVideoPool=[]);
  const safeNum=(v,d=0)=>Number.isFinite(Number(v))?Number(v):d;
  const clamp=(v,min,max)=>Math.max(min,Math.min(max,safeNum(v)));
  const activeVideosAt=t=>(Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).filter(ov=>ov&&ov.type==='video'&&!ov.reversePlayback&&
    Number(t)>=Number(ov.trimStart||0)&&Number(t)<Number(ov.trimEnd||0));
  const videoPoolRegister=el=>{if(el&&el.tagName==='VIDEO'&&!pool.includes(el)&&pool.length<POOL_MAX)pool.push(el);return el;};

  function contiguousSplit(prev,next){
    if(!prev||!next||prev.type!=='video'||next.type!=='video'||prev.reversePlayback||next.reversePlayback)return false;
    if(String(prev.src||'')!==String(next.src||''))return false;
    const timelineGap=Math.abs(Number(prev.trimEnd)-Number(next.trimStart));
    const prevSpeed=Math.max(.05,safeNum(prev.speed,1)),nextSpeed=Math.max(.05,safeNum(next.speed,1));
    const sourceEnd=safeNum(prev.sourceOffset)+(Number(prev.trimEnd)-Number(prev.trimStart))*prevSpeed;
    return timelineGap<=.025&&Math.abs(prevSpeed-nextSpeed)<=.001&&Math.abs(sourceEnd-safeNum(next.sourceOffset))<=.06;
  }
  function transferContinuousSplitVideos(t){
    if(!Array.isArray(overlays))return;
    const active=activeVideosAt(t);
    Object.keys(overlayMediaEls).forEach(oldId=>{
      const el=overlayMediaEls[oldId];if(!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO')return;
      const prev=overlays.find(ov=>ov&&ov.type==='video'&&String(ov.id)===String(oldId));
      if(!prev||Number(prev.trimEnd)>Number(t)+.025)return;
      const next=active.find(ov=>contiguousSplit(prev,ov));if(!next)return;
      const newId=String(next.id);
      if(overlayMediaEls[newId]&&overlayMediaEls[newId]!==el)return;
      const warm=overlayWarmEls[newId];
      if(warm&&warm!==el){delete overlayWarmEls[newId];releaseOverlayVideoEl(warm);}
      delete overlayMediaEls[oldId];overlayMediaEls[newId]=el;
      if(overlayChromaCanvases[oldId]){overlayChromaCanvases[newId]=overlayChromaCanvases[oldId];delete overlayChromaCanvases[oldId];}
      el.dataset.evV72OverlayId=newId;
      el.dataset.evClipSyncSignature=[String(next.src||''),Number(next.trimStart)||0,Number(next.trimEnd)||0,Number(next.sourceOffset)||0,Math.max(.05,Number(next.speed)||1)].join('|');
      delete el.dataset.evIncomingFrameWatch;delete el.dataset.evIncomingWatchId;
      if(typeof setupOverlayVideoEl==='function')setupOverlayVideoEl(el,next);
      if(window.__evVideoDebug)try{console.log('[evVideo] contiguous split decoder reused',oldId,'->',newId);}catch(_e){}
    });
  }

  // Pool-safe acquisition for active clips that have no pre-warmed element. Total live,
  // held and warmed HTMLVideoElements is capped at three.
  function acquirePooledVideo(ov,layer){
    let el=pool.find(node=>node&&node.dataset.evPoolFree==='1');
    if(!el&&pool.length<POOL_MAX){el=document.createElement('video');videoPoolRegister(el);}
    if(!el)return null;
    delete el.dataset.evPoolFree;delete el.dataset.evWarmElement;delete el.dataset.evWarmRole;delete el.dataset.evWarmTarget;
    if(typeof setupOverlayVideoEl==='function')setupOverlayVideoEl(el,ov);
    el.style.opacity='';el.style.visibility='hidden';el.style.pointerEvents='none';
    if(layer&&el.parentNode!==layer)layer.appendChild(el);
    return el;
  }
  const baseRender=window.renderOverlayMedia;
  if(typeof baseRender==='function'&&!baseRender.__evV165PoolRender){
    const wrapped=function(tSec){
      const time=Number.isFinite(Number(tSec))?Number(tSec):(Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0);
      transferContinuousSplitVideos(time);
      const layer=document.getElementById('overlayMediaLayer');
      const act=activeVideosAt(time);
      if(layer){
        Object.keys(overlayMediaEls).forEach(id=>videoPoolRegister(overlayMediaEls[id]));
        act.forEach(ov=>{
          const id=String(ov.id);
          if(overlayMediaEls[id])return;
          let el=overlayWarmEls[id];
          if(el){delete overlayWarmEls[id];delete el.dataset.evWarmElement;delete el.dataset.evWarmRole;delete el.dataset.evWarmTarget;}
          else el=acquirePooledVideo(ov,layer);
          if(el){
            overlayMediaEls[id]=el;
            if(typeof setupOverlayVideoEl==='function')setupOverlayVideoEl(el,ov);
            if(el.parentNode!==layer)layer.appendChild(el);
          }
        });
      }
      const result=baseRender.apply(this,arguments);
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV165PoolRender=true;
    wrapped.__evHeldOutgoing=baseRender.__evHeldOutgoing||{};
    window.renderOverlayMedia=wrapped;
  }

  // The forward-only queue occupies at most the two remaining pool slots while one decoder
  // is live. With no live decoder it can prepare up to three upcoming clips.
  window.warmUpcomingOverlayVideos=function(tSec){
    const now=performance.now();
    if(window.__evV165WarmAt&&now-window.__evV165WarmAt<90)return;
    window.__evV165WarmAt=now;
    const layer=document.getElementById('overlayMediaLayer');if(!layer||!Array.isArray(overlays))return;
    Object.keys(overlayMediaEls).forEach(id=>videoPoolRegister(overlayMediaEls[id]));
    const held=(window.renderOverlayMedia&&window.renderOverlayMedia.__evHeldOutgoing)||{};
    Object.keys(held).forEach(id=>{if(held[id]&&held[id].fallbackVideo)videoPoolRegister(held[id].fallbackVideo);});
    const activeRequired=activeVideosAt(Number(tSec)||0).length;
    const targetCount=Math.min(Math.max(0,POOL_MAX-activeRequired),activeRequired?POOL_WARM_TARGET:POOL_MAX);
    const time=Number(tSec)||0;
    const candidates=overlays.filter(ov=>ov&&ov.type==='video'&&window.evEffectiveVideoSrc(ov)&&!ov.reversePlayback&&
      Number(ov.trimEnd)>Number(ov.trimStart)&&Number(ov.trimStart)>time+.0005&&
      Number(ov.trimStart)-time<=POOL_AHEAD_SECONDS&&!overlayMediaEls[String(ov.id)])
      .sort((a,b)=>(Number(a.trimStart)||0)-(Number(b.trimStart)||0)).slice(0,targetCount);
    const wanted=new Set(candidates.map(ov=>String(ov.id)));
    Object.keys(overlayWarmEls).forEach(id=>{
      const el=overlayWarmEls[id],clip=overlays.find(ov=>ov&&String(ov.id)===id);
      if(!wanted.has(id)||!clip||String(el.dataset.evWarmSrc||'')!==window.evEffectiveVideoSrc(clip)){
        delete overlayWarmEls[id];releaseOverlayVideoEl(el);
      }
    });
    candidates.forEach(ov=>{
      const id=String(ov.id),target=Math.max(0,safeNum(ov.sourceOffset));
      let el=overlayWarmEls[id];
      if(el)return;
      el=pool.find(node=>node&&node.dataset.evPoolFree==='1');
      if(!el&&pool.length<POOL_MAX){el=document.createElement('video');videoPoolRegister(el);}
      if(!el)return;
      delete el.dataset.evPoolFree;
      el.dataset.evWarmElement='1';el.dataset.evWarmRole='next';el.dataset.evWarmTarget=String(target);
      el.style.visibility='hidden';el.style.opacity='0';el.style.pointerEvents='none';
      if(typeof setupOverlayVideoEl==='function')setupOverlayVideoEl(el,ov);
      if(el.parentNode!==layer)layer.appendChild(el);
      overlayWarmEls[id]=el;
    });
  };

  // ---------- Sample-scheduled unified audio ----------
  const AUDIO_HORIZON_MIN=.28;
  const AUDIO_MAX_DECODE_BYTES=100*1024*1024;
  const FADE_SEC=.005;
  // v199: audio is queued on the shared clock up to 3s ahead (window set by the preload
  // manager) so a late animation frame can never leave the next cut unscheduled.
  function audioHorizon(){const w=Number(window.__evPreloadWindowSec);return Math.max(AUDIO_HORIZON_MIN,Math.min(3,Number.isFinite(w)?w:1.5));}
  let anchor=null;
  const audioCache=window.__ethvelleDecodedAudioCache||(window.__ethvelleDecodedAudioCache=new Map());
  const scheduled=window.__ethvelleScheduledClipAudio||(window.__ethvelleScheduledClipAudio=new Map());
  const videoAudioNodes=window.__ethvelleVideoAudioNodes||(window.__ethvelleVideoAudioNodes={});
  const nativeLongVideoAudioIds=window.__ethvelleNativeLongVideoAudioIds||(window.__ethvelleNativeLongVideoAudioIds=new Set());
  function shouldUseNativeLongVideoAudio(ov){
    // Video audio stays on its own HTMLVideoElement so the soundtrack shares the exact
    // hardware-decoder clock with the picture. Buffer scheduling remains for standalone
    // audio clips; repeatedly decoding/composing compressed video sound caused crackle on
    // mobile devices, especially during long/high-bitrate clips.
    return !!(ov&&ov.type==='video'&&!ov.reversePlayback);
  }
  function hasSeparateAudioTwin(ov){
    if(!ov||!Array.isArray(overlays))return false;
    const t=Number(typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
    return overlays.some(other=>{
      if(!other||other===ov||other.type!=='audio'||String(other.src||'')!==String(ov.src||''))return false;
      if(t<Number(other.trimStart||0)||t>=Number(other.trimEnd||0)||other.muted===true)return false;
      const vol=clipVolume(other,t);
      if(!(Number(vol)>0))return false;
      const id=String(other.id),entry=scheduled.get(id);
      if(entry&&entry.run===runSerial)return true;
      const el=typeof audioOverlayEls!=='undefined'&&audioOverlayEls?audioOverlayEls[id]:null;
      return !!(el&&!el.paused&&!el.muted&&Number(el.volume)>0);
    });
  }
  function isNativeLongVideoAudio(ov){
    // A same-source audio clip suppresses the native video soundtrack only while that
    // replacement track is actually active and routed. Inactive, still-decoding or failed
    // twins must not leave the visible video silent.
    return !!(ov&&ov.type==='video'&&shouldUseNativeLongVideoAudio(ov)&&!hasSeparateAudioTwin(ov));
  }
  let runSerial=0;
  let lastTimelineTime=null;
  let lastTimelineContextTime=null;
  let lastPlaybackRateSig='';

  function audioContext(){
    if(typeof ensureAudioOverlayContext==='function')return ensureAudioOverlayContext();
    return null;
  }
  function loadDecodedAudio(src){
    const key=String(src||'');if(!key)return Promise.resolve({status:'failed',error:'empty source'});
    let entry=audioCache.get(key);if(entry)return entry.promise||Promise.resolve(entry);
    entry={status:'loading',buffer:null,error:null,promise:null};audioCache.set(key,entry);
    const ctx=audioContext();
    if(!ctx||typeof fetch!=='function'||typeof ctx.decodeAudioData!=='function'){
      entry.status='failed';entry.error='Web Audio decode unavailable';return Promise.resolve(entry);
    }
    entry.promise=fetch(key,{mode:'cors',credentials:'same-origin'}).then(response=>{
      if(!response.ok)throw new Error('Audio fetch HTTP '+response.status);
      const length=Number(response.headers&&response.headers.get('content-length'))||0;
      if(length>AUDIO_MAX_DECODE_BYTES)throw new Error('Source too large for in-memory audio decode');
      return response.arrayBuffer();
    }).then(bytes=>{
      if(bytes.byteLength>AUDIO_MAX_DECODE_BYTES)throw new Error('Source too large for in-memory audio decode');
      return ctx.decodeAudioData(bytes);
    }).then(buffer=>{
      if(!buffer||!buffer.length)throw new Error('Decoded source contains no audio samples');
      entry.status='ready';entry.buffer=buffer;entry.error=null;return entry;
    }).catch(error=>{
      entry.status='failed';entry.error=error&&error.message?error.message:String(error);
      if(window.__evVideoDebug)try{console.warn('[evAudio] buffer decode fallback',key,entry.error);}catch(_e){}
      return entry;
    });
    return entry.promise;
  }
  let lastPrimeAudioSignature='';
  function primeProjectAudio(){
    if(!Array.isArray(overlays))return;
    const audibles=overlays.filter(ov=>ov&&ov.src&&['video','audio'].includes(ov.type)&&!ov.reversePlayback);
    const signature=audibles.map(ov=>[
      String(ov.id),String(ov.src),ov.type,Number(ov.sourceDuration)||0,Number(ov.deviceMediaSize)||0,
      Number(ov.sourceOffset)||0,Number(ov.trimStart)||0,Number(ov.trimEnd)||0,Number(ov.speed)||1
    ].join('~')).join('|');
    if(signature===lastPrimeAudioSignature)return;
    lastPrimeAudioSignature=signature;
    audibles.forEach(ov=>{
      if(ov.type==='video'&&shouldUseNativeLongVideoAudio(ov)&&!hasSeparateAudioTwin(ov)){
        nativeLongVideoAudioIds.add(String(ov.id));
        // Do not fetch/decode video containers into a second audio clock. The native
        // soundtrack is synchronized by the same <video> decoder that supplies each frame.
        // This also avoids switching routes when an earlier buffer-cache entry becomes ready.
        stopScheduledOne(String(ov.id));
        return;
      }
      nativeLongVideoAudioIds.delete(String(ov.id));
      loadDecodedAudio(ov.src);
    });
    try{
      if(window.ethvellePitchSafeStretch)window.ethvellePitchSafeStretch.prewarm(ov=>{const st=audioCache.get(String(ov.src||''));return st&&st.status==='ready'?st.buffer:null;});
    }catch(_e){}
  }
  function clipRate(ov){
    const speed=Math.max(.05,safeNum(ov.speed,1));
    if(ov.type==='audio'&&typeof evAudioOverlayRate==='function')return evAudioOverlayRate(ov).rate;
    let pitch=0;
    try{const cfg=typeof getEthvelleAudioSettings==='function'?getEthvelleAudioSettings(ov):null;pitch=safeNum(cfg&&cfg.pitch,0);}catch(_e){}
    return speed*Math.pow(2,clamp(pitch,-12,12)/12);
  }
  function clipVolume(ov,t){
    const animated=typeof ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(ov,'volume',t):null;
    return clamp(animated==null?(ov.volume==null?1:ov.volume):animated,0,2);
  }
  function fadeAmount(ov,t){
    let cfg={};try{cfg=typeof getEthvelleAudioSettings==='function'?getEthvelleAudioSettings(ov)||{}:{};}catch(_e){}
    const duration=Math.max(.001,Number(ov.trimEnd)-Number(ov.trimStart));
    let fi=clamp(cfg.fadeIn,0,8),fo=clamp(cfg.fadeOut,0,8);
    if(fi+fo>duration){const k=duration/(fi+fo);fi*=k;fo*=k;}
    let amount=1;
    if(fi>0)amount=Math.min(amount,clamp((t-Number(ov.trimStart))/fi,0,1));
    if(fo>0)amount=Math.min(amount,clamp((Number(ov.trimEnd)-t)/fo,0,1));
    return amount;
  }
  function videoNodeFor(ov,ctx){
    const id=String(ov.id);let nodes=videoAudioNodes[id];
    if(!nodes){
      const input=ctx.createGain(),fade=ctx.createGain(),gain=ctx.createGain();
      let pan=null;try{pan=ctx.createStereoPanner?ctx.createStereoPanner():null;}catch(_e){}
      input.connect(fade);fade.connect(gain);
      if(pan){gain.connect(pan);pan.connect(ctx.destination);}else gain.connect(ctx.destination);
      nodes=videoAudioNodes[id]={input,fade,gain,pan,source:null,signature:String(ov.src||'')};
    }
    if(nodes.signature!==String(ov.src||'')){nodes.signature=String(ov.src||'');}
    return nodes;
  }
  function preferredVideoAt(timeSec){
    const t=Number(timeSec)||0;
    if(typeof evPreferredAudibleOverlayVideo==='function')return evPreferredAudibleOverlayVideo(t);
    for(let i=overlays.length-1;i>=0;i--){const ov=overlays[i];if(ov&&ov.type==='video'&&!ov.reversePlayback&&t>=Number(ov.trimStart||0)&&t<Number(ov.trimEnd||0))return ov;}
    return null;
  }
  function connectBufferSource(ov,source,ctx){
    if(ov.type==='audio'){
      let el=null;try{el=typeof evAudioOverlayEnsure==='function'?evAudioOverlayEnsure(ov):null;}catch(_e){}
      const nodes=audioOverlayNodes&&audioOverlayNodes[String(ov.id)];
      if(nodes){
        const graph=nodes.__evcaV66||nodes.__evcaDsp;
        const first=graph?(graph.hp||graph.highpass):nodes.gain;
        source.connect(first||ctx.destination);
        if(nodes.gain&&nodes.gain.gain){
          const v=clipVolume(ov,Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||Number(ov.trimStart)||0);
          try{nodes.gain.gain.setValueAtTime(v,ctx.currentTime);}catch(_e){nodes.gain.gain.value=v;}
        }
        if(nodes.__evcaV67&&nodes.__evcaV67.fade){
          const amount=fadeAmount(ov,Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||Number(ov.trimStart)||0);
          try{nodes.__evcaV67.fade.gain.setValueAtTime(amount,ctx.currentTime);}catch(_e){nodes.__evcaV67.fade.gain.value=amount;}
        }
        if(el){try{el.pause();el.dataset.evUnifiedBufferAudio='1';}catch(_e){}}
        return {nodes,el,route:'audio-graph'};
      }
      if(el){try{el.pause();el.dataset.evUnifiedBufferAudio='1';}catch(_e){}}
    }
    const nodes=videoNodeFor(ov,ctx);
    source.connect(nodes.input);
    return {nodes,route:'video-graph'};
  }
  function ensureAnchor(ctx){
    // One shared timeline->AudioContext mapping per play run. Every clip start/stop time is
    // derived from this single mapping, so the end of clip A and the start of clip B land on
    // the exact same sample instead of each being computed from a jittery per-frame reading.
    if(!anchor||anchor.run!==runSerial){
      const tl=(isPlaying&&typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function')?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():(Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0);
      anchor={ctx:ctx.currentTime,tl,lead:0,run:runSerial};
      try{if(window.__evPlaybackDiagnostics)window.__evPlaybackDiagnostics.recordAnchor();}catch(_e){}
    }
    return anchor;
  }
  function sameSourceContinuation(a,b){
    if(!a||!b||a===b||a.type!==b.type||a.reversePlayback||b.reversePlayback)return false;
    if(String(a.src||'')!==String(b.src||''))return false;
    const ra=clipRate(a),rb=clipRate(b);
    if(Math.abs(ra-rb)>.0005)return false;
    if(Math.abs((Number(a.trimEnd)||0)-(Number(b.trimStart)||0))>.006)return false;
    const endSrc=(Number(a.sourceOffset)||0)+((Number(a.trimEnd)||0)-(Number(a.trimStart)||0))*ra;
    if(Math.abs(endSrc-(Number(b.sourceOffset)||0))>.02)return false;
    if(Math.abs(clipVolume(a,Number(a.trimEnd)||0)-clipVolume(b,Number(b.trimStart)||0))>.01)return false;
    return true;
  }
  function audibleHere(ov,t){
    const st=audioCache.get(String(ov.src||''));
    if(!st||st.status!=='ready')return false;
    // v279: overlapping video layers are all audible (no single-owner rule).
    return true;
  }
  // The clip that continues the same waveform right before/after `ov` (a plain split). A cut
  // between two such halves must NOT be faded, otherwise a tiny dip would be audible.
  function continuationNeighbor(ov,dir){
    if(!Array.isArray(overlays))return null;
    for(let i=0;i<overlays.length;i++){
      const o=overlays[i];
      if(!o||o===ov||o.type!==ov.type)continue;
      if(dir<0){
        if(sameSourceContinuation(o,ov)&&audibleHere(o,Math.max(0,(Number(o.trimEnd)||0)-.001)))return o;
      }else if(sameSourceContinuation(ov,o)&&audibleHere(o,(Number(o.trimStart)||0)+.001))return o;
    }
    return null;
  }
  function stopScheduledOne(id){
    const entry=scheduled.get(String(id));if(!entry)return;
    scheduled.delete(String(id));
    let soft=false;
    try{
      const ctx=audioContext();
      // A source that is sounding right now is released with an ~8ms ramp, not chopped, so
      // pause / seek / edit never leaves a click at the stop point.
      if(ctx&&entry.env&&entry.when<=ctx.currentTime+.001){
        const t=ctx.currentTime,g=entry.env.gain;
        g.cancelScheduledValues(t);g.setValueAtTime(g.value,t);g.linearRampToValueAtTime(0,t+.008);
        entry.source.onended=null;entry.source.stop(t+.012);
        setTimeout(()=>{try{entry.source.disconnect();}catch(_e){}try{entry.env.disconnect();}catch(_e){}},80);
        soft=true;
      }
    }catch(_e){soft=false;}
    if(!soft){
      try{entry.source.onended=null;entry.source.stop();}catch(_e){}
      try{entry.source.disconnect();}catch(_e){}
      try{if(entry.env)entry.env.disconnect();}catch(_e){}
    }
    if(entry.route==='video-graph'){
      const nodes=videoAudioNodes[String(id)];
      if(nodes&&nodes.source===entry.source)nodes.source=null;
    }
  }
  function stopScheduledAll(reason){
    try{if(window.__evPlaybackDiagnostics)window.__evPlaybackDiagnostics.recordStop(reason||'other');}catch(_e){}
    Array.from(scheduled.keys()).forEach(stopScheduledOne);
    runSerial++;anchor=null;
    lastTimelineTime=null;lastTimelineContextTime=null;lastPlaybackRateSig='';
    internalRebufferHold=null;
  }
  // Internal decoder rebuffer: freeze the AudioContext and canonical timeline together.
  // Unlike a user pause, this deliberately does NOT stop, disconnect, or recreate any
  // scheduled AudioBufferSourceNode. AudioContext suspension preserves each source's sample.
  let internalRebufferHold=null;
  async function suspendForRebuffer(){
    if(internalRebufferHold)return internalRebufferHold;
    const ctx=audioContext();
    const startTime=(isPlaying&&typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function')
      ?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():(Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0);
    const hold={ctx,run:runSerial,time:startTime,suspendFailed:false};

    // Stop the performance-clock loop immediately, without entering the public pause wrapper
    // (the v165 public pause wrapper intentionally calls stopScheduledAll()).
    if(playbackRaf!==null){try{cancelAnimationFrame(playbackRaf);}catch(_e){}playbackRaf=null;}
    setCurrentAbsoluteTime(startTime);
    isPlaying=false;
    playbackStartedAtMs=0;
    playbackStartTimeSec=startTime;
    internalRebufferHold=hold;

    // Suspend first, then read currentTime after the suspend promise resolves. The context may
    // finish its current render quantum before stopping; this calculation advances the held
    // timeline to the exact audio sample at which the source nodes were frozen.
    if(ctx&&ctx.state==='running'){
      try{const pending=ctx.suspend();if(pending&&typeof pending.then==='function')await pending;}
      catch(err){hold.suspendFailed=true;if(window.__evVideoDebug)console.warn('[evAudio] rebuffer suspend:',err);}
    }
    // A real user pause/seek/edit may invalidate the run while suspend() is pending.
    // In that case the public action owns the clock and this internal hold must not rewind it.
    if(internalRebufferHold!==hold||runSerial!==hold.run)return hold;
    let frozen=startTime;
    if(ctx&&anchor&&anchor.run===hold.run&&Number.isFinite(ctx.currentTime)){
      frozen=anchor.tl+(ctx.currentTime-anchor.ctx-anchor.lead);
    }
    frozen=Math.max(0,Number(frozen)||0);
    setCurrentAbsoluteTime(frozen);
    playbackStartTimeSec=frozen;
    hold.time=frozen;
    try{if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();}catch(_e){}
    return hold;
  }
  async function resumeAfterRebuffer(){
    const hold=internalRebufferHold;
    const ctx=(hold&&hold.ctx)||audioContext();
    // Resume the same suspended context: previously scheduled sources continue at their
    // exact frozen sample. Do not call startPlayback(), which is a new-run rescheduler.
    if(ctx&&ctx.state==='suspended'){
      const pending=ctx.resume();if(pending&&typeof pending.then==='function')await pending;
    }
    let t=Math.max(0,Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||(hold&&hold.time)||0);
    // If the browser rejected suspend(), keep the clock aligned with the still-running
    // context at resume instead of replaying from an older timeline timestamp.
    if(hold&&hold.suspendFailed&&ctx&&ctx.state==='running'&&anchor&&anchor.run===hold.run){
      t=Math.max(0,anchor.tl+(ctx.currentTime-anchor.ctx-anchor.lead));
      setCurrentAbsoluteTime(t);
    }
    // Rebase both clocks at the just-resumed sample while keeping runSerial and all source
    // nodes intact. Future clip starts will now be scheduled from this matching clock pair.
    if(ctx){
      anchor={ctx:ctx.currentTime,tl:t,lead:0,run:runSerial,badTicks:0};
      try{if(window.__evPlaybackDiagnostics)window.__evPlaybackDiagnostics.recordAnchor();}catch(_e){}
      lastTimelineContextTime=ctx.currentTime;
    }else{
      anchor=null;
      lastTimelineContextTime=null;
    }
    lastTimelineTime=t;
    playbackStartTimeSec=t;
    playbackStartedAtMs=performance.now();
    isPlaying=true;
    internalRebufferHold=null;
    if(playbackRaf!==null){try{cancelAnimationFrame(playbackRaf);}catch(_e){}}
    playbackRaf=requestAnimationFrame(playbackTick);
    return true;
  }
  /*
   * v200 stop-path audit (all calls in this playback/audio scheduler were checked):
   * stopScheduledOne:
   * - scheduleBufferClip's existing-entry call only replaces a stale entry when its clip
   *   signature or playback-run token no longer matches; it is not a trim-boundary stop.
   * - syncScheduledAudio's preferred-video call evaluates preference within this clip's
   *   own trim span, so a following split cannot win merely because the playhead passed
   *   this clip's trimEnd.
   * - The scheduled-entry sweep immediately removes deleted clips, gives present clips
   *   trimEnd + 0.4s for their scheduled source to finish, and removes only stale entries
   *   lying before the active scheduling window.
   * stopScheduledAll:
   * - sync while playback is stopped; sustained clock-drift re-anchoring; starting a new
   *   playback run; pause; project-end stop; seek; speed edit; and audio-setting edit/reprime.
   *   These are transport/resync/edit invalidations, not automatic per-clip end cutoffs.
   */
  function prepareAudioClipElementFallback(ov){
    if(ov.type==='audio'&&typeof evAudioOverlayEnsure==='function'){
      try{const el=evAudioOverlayEnsure(ov);if(el)delete el.dataset.evUnifiedBufferAudio;}catch(_e){}
    }
    if(ov.type==='video'){
      const el=overlayMediaEls&&overlayMediaEls[String(ov.id)];
      if(el&&el.tagName==='VIDEO'){
        delete el.dataset.evUnifiedBufferAudio;
        // A failed decode is the only route on which this video element may emit its own audio.
        if(typeof evOverlayVideoIsAudibleAt==='function'&&evOverlayVideoIsAudibleAt(ov,currentAbsoluteTimeSec)&&isPlaying){el.muted=false;el.volume=clamp(clipVolume(ov,currentAbsoluteTimeSec),0,1);el.removeAttribute('muted');}
      }
    }
  }
  function applyVideoMuteState(){
    if(!Array.isArray(overlays))return;
    overlays.forEach(ov=>{
      if(!ov||ov.type!=='video'||ov.reversePlayback)return;
      const el=overlayMediaEls&&overlayMediaEls[String(ov.id)];
      if(!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO')return;
      if(isNativeLongVideoAudio(ov)){
        const now=Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
        const inClip=!!(isPlaying&&now>=Number(ov.trimStart||0)&&now<Number(ov.trimEnd||0));
        if(inClip&&el.isConnected&&el.readyState>=2&&!el.paused&&!el.seeking&&el.dataset.evPlayingEventSeen==='1'){
          delete el.dataset.evUnifiedBufferAudio;
          // The V140 ramp owns the MediaElementSource gain, volume automation, topmost-layer
          // preference and decoder checks (including muting lower overlapping video layers).
          if(typeof window.evRampOverlayVideoAudible==='function')window.evRampOverlayVideoAudible(el,ov);
        }else{
          if(el.__evGainRampRaf){try{cancelAnimationFrame(el.__evGainRampRaf);}catch(_e){}el.__evGainRampRaf=0;}
          delete el.dataset.evGainRampActive;
          try{el.muted=true;el.defaultMuted=true;el.volume=0;el.setAttribute('muted','');}catch(_e){}
          delete el.dataset.evUnifiedBufferAudio;
        }
        return;
      }
      const state=audioCache.get(String(ov.src||''));
      if(state&&state.status==='failed'){
        delete el.dataset.evUnifiedBufferAudio;
        if(isPlaying){
          if(typeof evOverlayVideoIsAudibleAt==='function'&&evOverlayVideoIsAudibleAt(ov,currentAbsoluteTimeSec)){el.muted=false;el.removeAttribute('muted');el.volume=clamp(clipVolume(ov,currentAbsoluteTimeSec),0,1);}
          else{el.muted=true;el.volume=0;el.setAttribute('muted','');}
        }else{el.muted=true;el.volume=0;el.setAttribute('muted','');}
      }else{
        el.dataset.evUnifiedBufferAudio='1';
        if(!el.muted||el.volume!==0){el.muted=true;el.volume=0;el.setAttribute('muted','');}
      }
    });
  }
  function scheduleBufferClip(ov,buffer,ctx,time){
    const id=String(ov.id),rate=clipRate(ov);
    const clipStart=Number(ov.trimStart)||0,clipEnd=Number(ov.trimEnd)||0;
    let timelineStart=Math.max(time,clipStart);
    if(timelineStart>=clipEnd)return;
    const signature=[String(ov.src||''),clipStart,clipEnd,Number(ov.sourceOffset)||0,rate,ov.type].join('|');
    const existing=scheduled.get(id);
    if(existing&&existing.signature===signature&&existing.run===runSerial){
      // Already queued on the shared clock: only keep its live gain/fade/pan values current.
      const vol=clipVolume(ov,timelineStart);
      if(existing.route==='video-graph'){
        const n=existing.nodes;
        try{n.gain.gain.setTargetAtTime(vol,ctx.currentTime,.015);}catch(_e){n.gain.gain.value=vol;}
        if(n.fade){const fade=fadeAmount(ov,timelineStart);try{n.fade.gain.setTargetAtTime(fade,ctx.currentTime,.015);}catch(_e){n.fade.gain.value=fade;}}
        if(n.pan){let cfg={};try{cfg=typeof getEthvelleAudioSettings==='function'?getEthvelleAudioSettings(ov)||{}:{};}catch(_e){}try{n.pan.pan.setTargetAtTime(clamp(cfg.pan,-100,100)/100,ctx.currentTime,.02);}catch(_e){}}
      }else if(existing.route==='audio-graph'){
        const n=existing.nodes;
        if(n&&n.gain&&n.gain.gain){try{n.gain.gain.setTargetAtTime(vol,ctx.currentTime,.015);}catch(_e){n.gain.gain.value=vol;}}
        if(n&&n.__evcaV67&&n.__evcaV67.fade){const fade=fadeAmount(ov,timelineStart);try{n.__evcaV67.fade.gain.setTargetAtTime(fade,ctx.currentTime,.015);}catch(_e){n.__evcaV67.fade.gain.value=fade;}}
      }
      return;
    }else if(existing)stopScheduledOne(id);

    const A=ensureAnchor(ctx);
    let when=A.ctx+A.lead+(timelineStart-A.tl);
    let offset=Math.max(0,Number(ov.sourceOffset)||0)+(timelineStart-clipStart)*rate;
    let sourceDuration=Math.min((clipEnd-timelineStart)*rate,buffer.duration-offset);
    const nowCtx=ctx.currentTime+.003;
    if(when<nowCtx){
      // Scheduler ran late (frame hitch): skip the part that should already have played
      // instead of starting it from its beginning, which would put it behind the picture.
      const late=nowCtx-when;
      offset+=late*rate;sourceDuration-=late*rate;timelineStart+=late;when=nowCtx;
    }
    if(offset>=buffer.duration-.005||sourceDuration<=.005)return;

    /* v221: clip speed must not change pitch. Use a time-stretched copy (pitch untouched) and play it at the
       pitch-setting rate only; timeline mapping below stays in original-source seconds. */
    let playBuffer=buffer,playRate=rate,bufScale=1,bufOrigin=0;
    const stretchSpeed=Math.max(.05,Number(ov.speed)||1);
    if(Math.abs(stretchSpeed-1)>.001&&window.ethvellePitchSafeStretch){
      const stretched=window.ethvellePitchSafeStretch.getReady(buffer,stretchSpeed,Math.max(0,Number(ov.sourceOffset)||0),(Number(ov.sourceOffset)||0)+(clipEnd-clipStart)*rate+.1);
      if(stretched===null)return; /* still computing: stay silent for a moment, the next sync tick schedules it */
      if(stretched&&stretched.buffer){playBuffer=stretched.buffer;playRate=rate/stretchSpeed;bufScale=1/stretchSpeed;bufOrigin=stretched.originSec;}
    }
    const src=ctx.createBufferSource();try{if(window.__evPlaybackDiagnostics)window.__evPlaybackDiagnostics.recordSourceNode();}catch(_e){}src.buffer=playBuffer;src.playbackRate.value=playRate;
    // Per-source envelope: a few-ms fade in/out at every real cut removes the click you hear
    // when a clip is cut through the middle of a waveform. Plain splits of the same source
    // (continuous audio) are detected and left unfaded so they stay seamless.
    const env=ctx.createGain();src.connect(env);
    const route=connectBufferSource(ov,env,ctx);
    if(route.route==='video-graph'){
      const n=route.nodes;
      try{n.gain.gain.setValueAtTime(clipVolume(ov,timelineStart),when);}catch(_e){n.gain.gain.value=clipVolume(ov,timelineStart);}
      if(n.fade){const fade=fadeAmount(ov,timelineStart);try{n.fade.gain.setValueAtTime(fade,when);}catch(_e){n.fade.gain.value=fade;}}
      if(n.pan){let cfg={};try{cfg=typeof getEthvelleAudioSettings==='function'?getEthvelleAudioSettings(ov)||{}:{};}catch(_e){}try{n.pan.pan.setValueAtTime(clamp(cfg.pan,-100,100)/100,when);}catch(_e){}}
    }else if(route.nodes&&route.nodes.gain&&route.nodes.gain.gain){
      const gain=route.nodes.gain.gain;
      try{gain.setValueAtTime(clipVolume(ov,timelineStart),when);}catch(_e){gain.value=clipVolume(ov,timelineStart);}
      if(route.nodes.__evcaV67&&route.nodes.__evcaV67.fade){const f=route.nodes.__evcaV67.fade.gain;try{f.setValueAtTime(fadeAmount(ov,timelineStart),when);}catch(_e){f.value=fadeAmount(ov,timelineStart);}}
    }
    try{
      const wall=sourceDuration/rate,endAt=when+wall,f=Math.min(FADE_SEC,wall/3);
      const prev=continuationNeighbor(ov,-1),next=continuationNeighbor(ov,1);
      const joinsPrev=!!prev&&timelineStart<=clipStart+.004&&scheduled.has(String(prev.id));
      const g=env.gain;
      if(!joinsPrev){g.setValueAtTime(0,when);g.linearRampToValueAtTime(1,when+f);}
      if(!next&&wall>f*3){g.setValueAtTime(1,endAt-f);g.linearRampToValueAtTime(0,endAt);}
    }catch(_e){}
    const entry={source:src,env,signature,run:runSerial,when,timelineStart,rate,route:route.route,nodes:route.nodes};
    scheduled.set(id,entry);
    src.onended=()=>{if(scheduled.get(id)===entry)scheduled.delete(id);try{src.disconnect();}catch(_e){}try{env.disconnect();}catch(_e){}};
    try{src.start(when,Math.max(0,(offset-bufOrigin)*bufScale),Math.max(.001,sourceDuration*bufScale));}catch(err){
      scheduled.delete(id);try{src.disconnect();}catch(_e){}try{env.disconnect();}catch(_e){}
      const failed=audioCache.get(String(ov.src||''));if(failed){failed.status='failed';failed.error='AudioBufferSource scheduling failed: '+(err&&err.message?err.message:String(err));}
      if(window.__evVideoDebug)console.warn('[evAudio] buffer schedule fallback',id,err);
      prepareAudioClipElementFallback(ov);
    }
  }
  function syncScheduledAudio(){
    if(!isPlaying){if(scheduled.size)stopScheduledAll('pause');applyVideoMuteState();return;}
    const ctx=audioContext();if(!ctx)return;
    if(ctx.state==='suspended'){try{ctx.resume().catch(()=>{});}catch(_e){}}
    const time=Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
    const H=audioHorizon();
    // Distinguish a true seek from a delayed rAF. During a frame hitch, both the project
    // clock and AudioContext clock advance together, so pre-scheduled samples must keep
    // running rather than being stopped/restarted merely because one UI frame arrived late.
    // v199: compare the exact (performance.now based) timeline clock with the audio clock
    // through the single run anchor. A hitch moves both clocks together and is ignored; only a
    // real separation (seek while playing, suspended context) re-anchors. One noisy reading
    // is never enough: it has to persist for two checks, so a late frame can't cause a glitch.
    if(anchor&&anchor.run===runSerial&&isPlaying&&typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'){
      const expected=anchor.tl+(ctx.currentTime-anchor.ctx-anchor.lead);
      const drift=Math.abs(getCurrentAbsoluteTime()-expected-anchor.lead);
      if(drift>.5||(drift>.12&&anchor.badTicks>=1)){stopScheduledAll('drift');}
      else anchor.badTicks=drift>.12?(anchor.badTicks||0)+1:0;
    }
    lastTimelineTime=time;lastTimelineContextTime=ctx.currentTime;
    if(!Array.isArray(overlays))return;
    // Keep native elements silent while a source is loading or decoded. Only a confirmed
    // decode failure/oversize result re-enables the clip's own media-element audio.
    overlays.slice().sort((x,y)=>(Number(x&&x.trimStart)||0)-(Number(y&&y.trimStart)||0)).forEach(ov=>{
      if(!ov||!['video','audio'].includes(ov.type)||ov.reversePlayback||!ov.src)return;
      if(isNativeLongVideoAudio(ov)){
        stopScheduledOne(ov.id);
        const el=overlayMediaEls&&overlayMediaEls[String(ov.id)];
        const active=!!(time>=Number(ov.trimStart||0)&&time<Number(ov.trimEnd||0));
        if(el&&el.tagName==='VIDEO'){
          if(active&&el.isConnected&&el.readyState>=2&&!el.paused&&!el.seeking&&el.dataset.evPlayingEventSeen==='1'){
            delete el.dataset.evUnifiedBufferAudio;
            if(typeof window.evRampOverlayVideoAudible==='function')window.evRampOverlayVideoAudible(el,ov);
          }else{
            try{el.muted=true;el.defaultMuted=true;el.volume=0;el.setAttribute('muted','');}catch(_e){}
            delete el.dataset.evUnifiedBufferAudio;
          }
        }
        return;
      }
      const state=audioCache.get(String(ov.src));
      if(!state||state.status==='loading'){
        if(ov.type==='video'){
          const el=overlayMediaEls&&overlayMediaEls[String(ov.id)];
          if(el&&el.tagName==='VIDEO'){el.muted=true;el.volume=0;el.setAttribute('muted','');el.dataset.evUnifiedBufferAudio='1';}
        }else{
          const el=audioOverlayEls&&audioOverlayEls[String(ov.id)];
          if(el){try{el.pause();el.dataset.evUnifiedBufferAudio='1';}catch(_e){}}
        }
        return;
      }
      if(state.status==='failed'){prepareAudioClipElementFallback(ov);return;}
      if(state.status==='ready'){
        if(ov.type==='video'){
          // v279: no single-owner rule any more; every active video layer keeps its own sound.
          const el=overlayMediaEls&&overlayMediaEls[String(ov.id)];
          if(el&&el.tagName==='VIDEO'){el.dataset.evUnifiedBufferAudio='1';if(!el.muted||el.volume!==0){el.muted=true;el.volume=0;el.setAttribute('muted','');}}
        }else{
          const el=audioOverlayEls&&audioOverlayEls[String(ov.id)];
          if(el){try{el.pause();el.dataset.evUnifiedBufferAudio='1';}catch(_e){}}
        }
        if(Number(ov.trimEnd)>time&&Number(ov.trimStart)<time+H){
          try{scheduleBufferClip(ov,state.buffer,ctx,time);}catch(err){
            state.status='failed';state.error='Audio scheduling failed: '+(err&&err.message?err.message:String(err));
            prepareAudioClipElementFallback(ov);
            if(window.__evVideoDebug)console.warn('[evAudio] scheduling error',ov.id,err);
          }
        }
      }
    });
    // Stop sources whose clips have ended or were deleted; retain future scheduled nodes.
    Array.from(scheduled.keys()).forEach(id=>{
      const entry=scheduled.get(id),ov=overlays.find(item=>item&&String(item.id)===id);
      if(!ov||time>=Number(ov.trimEnd)+.4||time<Number(ov.trimStart)-H-.05)stopScheduledOne(id);
    });
    applyVideoMuteState();
  }

  // Pre-decode all clip sources during editing/paused preview, not at the exact cut.
  const baseRenderAudio=window.renderAudioOverlayMedia;
  if(typeof baseRenderAudio==='function'&&!baseRenderAudio.__evV165Prime){
    const wrapped=function(){try{primeProjectAudio();}catch(_e){}return baseRenderAudio.apply(this,arguments);};
    wrapped.__evV165Prime=true;window.renderAudioOverlayMedia=wrapped;
  }
  const baseRenderMedia=window.renderOverlayMedia;
  if(typeof baseRenderMedia==='function'&&!baseRenderMedia.__evV165Prime){
    const wrapped=function(){try{primeProjectAudio();}catch(_e){}return baseRenderMedia.apply(this,arguments);};
    wrapped.__evV165Prime=true;
    wrapped.__evHeldOutgoing=baseRenderMedia.__evHeldOutgoing||{};
    window.renderOverlayMedia=wrapped;
  }
  // The media-element fallback must stay silent whenever a cached AudioBuffer is available
  // or is still decoding; the only fallback is an explicit fetch/decode failure.
  const originalAudioStart=window.evAudioOverlayStart||evAudioOverlayStart;
  if(typeof originalAudioStart==='function'&&!originalAudioStart.__evV165Unified){
    const wrapped=function(ov,el,time){
      const state=audioCache.get(String(ov&&ov.src||''));
      if(state&&state.status!=='failed'){
        if(el&&!el.paused){try{el.pause();}catch(_e){}}
        if(el){el.dataset.evUnifiedBufferAudio='1';}
        return;
      }
      return originalAudioStart.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evV165Unified=true;window.evAudioOverlayStart=wrapped;
    // The lexical function binding is updated so renderAudioOverlayMedia uses this gate.
    try{evAudioOverlayStart=wrapped;}catch(_e){}
  }
  const baseRamp=window.evRampOverlayVideoAudible;
  if(typeof baseRamp==='function'&&!baseRamp.__evV165Unified){
    const wrapped=function(el,ov){
      const nativeVideo=!!(ov&&ov.type==='video'&&nativeLongVideoAudioIds.has(String(ov.id))&&!hasSeparateAudioTwin(ov));
      const state=audioCache.get(String(ov&&ov.src||''));
      if(!nativeVideo&&state&&state.status!=='failed'){
        if(el){el.muted=true;el.defaultMuted=true;el.volume=0;el.setAttribute('muted','');el.dataset.evUnifiedBufferAudio='1';}
        return false;
      }
      return baseRamp.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evV165Unified=true;window.evRampOverlayVideoAudible=wrapped;
    try{evRampOverlayVideoAudible=wrapped;}catch(_e){}
  }

  // Start/resume AudioContext synchronously within the Play gesture, then schedule the first
  // active and near-future sources against the same performance-anchored timeline clock.
  const originalStart=window.startPlayback;
  if(typeof originalStart==='function'&&!originalStart.__evV165Audio){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const ctx=audioContext();if(ctx&&ctx.state==='suspended'){try{ctx.resume().catch(()=>{});}catch(_e){}}
      try{primeProjectAudio();}catch(_e){}
      stopScheduledAll('start');
      const result=originalStart.apply(this,arguments);
      runSerial++;lastTimelineTime=null;
      try{syncScheduledAudio();}catch(_e){}
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV165Audio=true;window.startPlayback=wrapped;
    try{startPlayback=wrapped;}catch(_e){}
  }
  const originalPause=window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime;
  if(typeof originalPause==='function'&&!originalPause.__evV165Audio){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=originalPause.apply(this,arguments);stopScheduledAll('pause');return result;};
    wrapped.__evV165Audio=true;window.pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime=wrapped;
    try{pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime=wrapped;}catch(_e){}
  }
  const originalStop=window.stopPlaybackAtProjectEnd;
  if(typeof originalStop==='function'&&!originalStop.__evV165Audio){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=originalStop.apply(this,arguments);stopScheduledAll('other');return result;};
    wrapped.__evV165Audio=true;window.stopPlaybackAtProjectEnd=wrapped;
    try{stopPlaybackAtProjectEnd=wrapped;}catch(_e){}
  }
  const originalTick=window.playbackTick;
  if(typeof originalTick==='function'&&!originalTick.__evV165Audio){
    const wrapped=function(timestamp){
      const result=originalTick.apply(this,arguments);
      try{syncScheduledAudio();}catch(err){if(window.__evVideoDebug)console.warn('[evAudio] clock scheduler:',err);}
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV165Audio=true;window.playbackTick=wrapped;
    try{playbackTick=wrapped;}catch(_e){}
  }


  const originalSeek=window.seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds;
  if(typeof originalSeek==='function'&&!originalSeek.__evV165Audio){
    const wrapped=function(){
      stopScheduledAll('edit');
      const result=originalSeek.apply(this,arguments);
      if(isPlaying){
        // A programmatic seek during playback becomes the new performance-clock anchor;
        // otherwise the next rAF would snap back to the pre-seek position.
        playbackStartTimeSec=Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
        playbackStartedAtMs=performance.now();
        lastTimelineTime=null;lastTimelineContextTime=null;
        try{syncScheduledAudio();}catch(_e){}
      }
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV165Audio=true;window.seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds=wrapped;
    try{seekAbsoluteTimelineSeconds=wrapped;}catch(_e){}
  }
  const originalSpeedEdit=window.speedPanelSetSpeed;
  if(typeof originalSpeedEdit==='function'&&!originalSpeedEdit.__evV165Audio){
    const wrapped=function(){
      stopScheduledAll('edit');
      const result=originalSpeedEdit.apply(this,arguments);
      try{primeProjectAudio();if(isPlaying)syncScheduledAudio();}catch(_e){}
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evV165Audio=true;window.speedPanelSetSpeed=wrapped;
  }

  // Reprime after edits, source replacement, trims and speed changes. Reusing a URL cache
  // prevents repeated fetch/decode for split clips that share the same file.
  const originalSetter=window.setEthvelleAudioSetting;
  if(typeof originalSetter==='function'&&!originalSetter.__evV165Audio){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=originalSetter.apply(this,arguments);try{stopScheduledAll('edit');primeProjectAudio();if(isPlaying)syncScheduledAudio();}catch(_e){}return result;};
    wrapped.__evV165Audio=true;window.setEthvelleAudioSetting=wrapped;
  }

  window.__ethvelleAudioSchedulerDiagnostics={
    cache:audioCache,scheduled,
    getPoolState:()=>({size:pool.length,free:pool.filter(el=>el&&el.dataset.evPoolFree==='1').length,busy:pool.filter(el=>el&&el.dataset.evPoolFree!=='1').length}),
    getContext:audioContext,
    getPlaybackState:()=>({playing:!!isPlaying,time:Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0}),
    getProjectAudioSources:()=>Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.filter(o=>o&&['video','audio'].includes(o.type)&&o.src).map(o=>({id:String(o.id),type:String(o.type),src:String(o.src)})):[],
    getNativeVideoAudioIds:()=>Array.from(nativeLongVideoAudioIds),
    stop:stopScheduledAll,sync:syncScheduledAudio,prime:primeProjectAudio,load:loadDecodedAudio,horizon:audioHorizon,anchor:()=>anchor,
    suspendForRebuffer,resumeAfterRebuffer
  };
  // Prime once after the app's clip stores and media graph have been initialized.
  try{primeProjectAudio();}catch(_e){}
})();
</script>

<script id="capExportScript">
(function(){
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  let target=0,shown=0,raf=0,frameW=9,frameH=16,last=null;
  const prev=$('capPrev'),pctEl=$('capPct'),frame=$('capFrame');
  function paint(){
    shown+=(target-shown)*0.18; if(Math.abs(target-shown)<0.05)shown=target;
    pctEl.textContent=(shown>=100?'100':shown.toFixed(1))+'%';
    frame.style.setProperty('--p',shown);
    raf=(shown!==target&&$('capExp').classList.contains('show'))?requestAnimationFrame(paint):0;
  }
  function setPrevSize(w,h){
    if(!w||!h)return; frameW=w;frameH=h;
    const pw=270,ph=Math.max(2,Math.round(pw*h/w)); if(prev.width!==pw||prev.height!==ph){prev.width=pw;prev.height=ph;}
    // keep very tall frames inside the screen
    const landscape=w>=h,maxW=landscape?Math.min(window.innerWidth*0.84,440):Math.min(window.innerWidth*0.58,300);
    frame.style.width=Math.min(maxW,(window.innerHeight*0.52)*w/h)+'px';
  }
  window.__capExportBegin=function(w,h){
    if(last&&last.url){try{URL.revokeObjectURL(last.url);}catch(e){} last=null;}
    $('capDone').classList.remove('show');
    const v=$('capVideo');try{v.pause();v.removeAttribute('src');v.load();}catch(e){}
    target=0;shown=0;frame.style.setProperty('--p',0);setPrevSize(w||9,h||16);
    const c=prev.getContext('2d');c.fillStyle='#111';c.fillRect(0,0,prev.width,prev.height);
  };
  window.__capExportFrame=function(cv){
    try{ if(cv.width!==frameW||cv.height!==frameH)setPrevSize(cv.width,cv.height);
      prev.getContext('2d').drawImage(cv,0,0,prev.width,prev.height);}catch(e){}
  };
  window.showExportProgress=function(pct){
    target=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(pct)||0));
    $('capExp').classList.add('show');
    if(!raf)raf=requestAnimationFrame(paint);
  };
  window.hideExportProgress=function(){$('capExp').classList.remove('show');};
  $('capCancel').onclick=function(){
    if(confirm('Stop exporting? Your credits will be refunded.')){window.__capExportCancelled=true;}
  };

  function fmtSize(b){return b>1048576?(b/1048576).toFixed(1)+' MB':Math.max(1,Math.round(b/1024))+' KB';}
  function fmtDur(s){s=Math.round(s||0);return String(Math.floor(s/60)).padStart(2,'0')+':'+String(s%60).padStart(2,'0');}
  window.__capExportDone=function(info){
    last=info;
    let dur=0;try{dur=getExportContentDurationSec();}catch(e){}
    const q=(typeof exportQuality!=='undefined'?exportQuality:720);
    $('capMeta').textContent=[q===2160?'4K':q+'p',(Number(window.exportFrameRate)||30)+' fps',fmtDur(dur),fmtSize(info.blob.size)].join(' · ');
    const v=$('capVideo');v.src=info.url;v.currentTime=0.01;
    const box=$('capVid');box.classList.remove('playing');
    target=100;shown=100;pctEl.textContent='100%';
    $('capExp').classList.remove('show');
    $('capDone').classList.add('show');$('capDone').scrollTop=0;
  };
  function closeDone(){const v=$('capVideo');try{v.pause();}catch(e){}$('capDone').classList.remove('show');}
  $('capDoneBtn').onclick=closeDone;$('capDoneBack').onclick=closeDone;$('capQEdit').onclick=closeDone;
  $('capVid').onclick=function(){const v=$('capVideo'),b=$('capVid');if(v.paused){v.play();b.classList.add('playing');}else{v.pause();b.classList.remove('playing');}};
  $('capVideo').onended=function(){$('capVid').classList.remove('playing');};
  function saveAgain(){
    if(!last)return;const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=last.url;a.download=last.filename;document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();
    if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Saved to device',{duration:1600});
  }
  $('capSave').onclick=saveAgain;
  async function shareVideo(){
    if(!last)return;
    try{
      const file=new File([last.blob],last.filename,{type:last.blob.type});
      if(navigator.canShare&&navigator.canShare({files:[file]})){await navigator.share({files:[file],title:last.filename});return;}
    }catch(e){if(e&&e.name==='AbortError')return;}
    if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Sharing isn\u2019t supported here — video saved to your device',{duration:2400});
    saveAgain();
  }
  $('capShare').onclick=shareVideo;
  document.querySelectorAll('#capDone [data-share]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=shareVideo);
  $('capQNew').onclick=function(){closeDone();try{exitEditor();}catch(e){}};
  const openOpts=function(){closeDone();setTimeout(()=>{try{openExportSheet();}catch(e){}},50);};
  $('capQOpts').onclick=openOpts;$('capQQual').onclick=openOpts;
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v221-pitch-safe-speed-js">
/* Ethvelle v221: pitch-safe clip speed.
   Speeding up or slowing down a clip used to play its audio buffer with a faster/slower
   playbackRate, which also raises/lowers the pitch. This module time-stretches the decoded audio
   (WSOLA: waveform-similarity overlap-add) so the speed changes but the pitch stays the same.
   Live playback and export both use it. Results are cached, computed in small async slices so
   the UI never freezes, and stale jobs are dropped when the speed changes again. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.ethvellePitchSafeStretch)return;
  const MAX_ENTRIES=5,FULL_BUFFER_MAX_SEC=150,PAD_SEC=4,MAX_RANGE_SEC=900;
  const cache=[];
  const nextSlice=()=>new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,0));

  /* Pure WSOLA core: channels = Float32Array[], returns Float32Array[] of length round(inLen/speed). */
  async function wsola(chans,sr,speed,shouldAbort){
    const nch=chans.length,inLen=chans[0].length,D=4;
    let N=Math.round(sr*0.046/(2*D))*(2*D);N=Math.max(256,N);
    const Hs=N/2,Ha=Hs*speed,tol=Math.max(D*8,Math.round(sr*0.012/D)*D);
    const outLen=Math.max(1,Math.round(inLen/speed));
    const out=chans.map(()=>new Float32Array(outLen+N+4));
    const decLen=Math.floor(inLen/D);
    const dec=new Float32Array(decLen+N/D+8);
    for(let i=0;i<decLen;i++){let s=0;const b=i*D;for(let c=0;c<nch;c++){const ch=chans[c];s+=ch[b]+ch[b+1]+ch[b+2]+ch[b+3];}dec[i]=s/(4*nch);}
    const win=new Float32Array(N);for(let i=0;i<N;i++)win[i]=0.5-0.5*Math.cos(2*Math.PI*i/N);
    const L=Hs/D,frames=Math.ceil(outLen/Hs)+1,ch0=chans[0];
    let prev=0,lastYield=(typeof performance!=='undefined'?performance.now():Date.now());
    for(let k=0;k<frames;k++){
      let pos=0;
      if(k>0){
        const target=Math.round(k*Ha);
        pos=Math.min(target,Math.max(0,inLen-1));
        const refStart=prev+Hs,r0=Math.floor(refStart/D);
        if(r0+L<=decLen){
          let lo=Math.max(0,target-tol),hi=Math.min(target+tol,inLen-1);
          lo=Math.ceil(lo/D)*D;
          let best=-Infinity,bestP=pos;
          for(let p=lo;p<=hi;p+=D){
            const q=p/D;if(q+L>decLen)break;
            let dot=0,en=0;
            for(let j=0;j<L;j++){const a=dec[q+j];dot+=a*dec[r0+j];en+=a*a;}
            const score=dot/Math.sqrt(en+1e-6);
            if(score>best){best=score;bestP=p;}
          }
          /* fine alignment at full resolution (channel 0, +-D samples) */
          let fineBest=-Infinity,fineP=bestP;
          if(refStart+Hs<=inLen){
            for(let p=Math.max(0,bestP-D);p<=Math.min(hi,bestP+D);p++){
              if(p+Hs>inLen)break;
              let dot=0,en=0;
              for(let j=0;j<Hs;j+=2){const a=ch0[p+j];dot+=a*ch0[refStart+j];en+=a*a;}
              const score=dot/Math.sqrt(en+1e-6);
              if(score>fineBest){fineBest=score;fineP=p;}
            }
          }
          pos=fineP;
        }
      }
      const base=k*Hs;
      for(let c=0;c<nch;c++){
        const ch=chans[c],o=out[c];
        for(let i=0;i<N;i++){
          const idx=pos+i;if(idx>=inLen)break;
          const w=(k===0&&i<Hs)?1:win[i];
          o[base+i]+=ch[idx]*w;
        }
      }
      prev=pos;
      if((k&15)===15){
        const now=(typeof performance!=='undefined'?performance.now():Date.now());
        if(now-lastYield>14){await nextSlice();lastYield=(typeof performance!=='undefined'?performance.now():Date.now());if(shouldAbort&&shouldAbort())return null;}
      }
    }
    return out.map(o=>o.subarray(0,outLen));
  }

  function makeBuffer(nch,len,sr){
    try{return new AudioBuffer({numberOfChannels:nch,length:len,sampleRate:sr});}
    catch(_e){const C=window.OfflineAudioContext||window.webkitOfflineAudioContext;return new C(nch,Math.max(1,len),sr).createBuffer(nch,len,sr);}
  }
  function liveWanted(entry){
    try{
      if(typeof overlays==='undefined'||!Array.isArray(overlays))return true;
      return overlays.some(o=>o&&(o.type==='video'||o.type==='audio')&&Math.abs((Number(o.speed)||1)-entry.speed)<.0005);
    }catch(_e){return true;}
  }
  async function runJob(entry,transient){
    const buf=entry.src,sr=buf.sampleRate;
    const s0=Math.max(0,Math.floor(entry.start*sr)),s1=Math.min(buf.length,Math.ceil(entry.end*sr));
    if(s1-s0<64){entry.failed=true;return null;}
    const chans=[];for(let c=0;c<buf.numberOfChannels;c++)chans.push(buf.getChannelData(c).subarray(s0,s1));
    const res=await wsola(chans,sr,entry.speed,transient?null:()=>entry.dead||!liveWanted(entry));
    if(!res){entry.dead=true;return null;}
    const out=makeBuffer(res.length,res[0].length,sr);
    for(let c=0;c<res.length;c++){if(out.copyToChannel)out.copyToChannel(res[c],c,0);else out.getChannelData(c).set(res[c]);}
    entry.out=out;entry.originSec=s0/sr;entry.start=s0/sr;entry.end=s1/sr;
    return entry;
  }
  let queue=Promise.resolve();
  function enqueue(entry,transient){
    const job=()=>runJob(entry,transient).catch(err=>{entry.failed=true;try{console.warn('Ethvelle pitch-safe stretch failed:',err);}catch(_e){}return null;});
    if(transient)return job();
    entry.promise=queue.then(()=>{if(entry.dead||!liveWanted(entry)){entry.dead=true;return null;}return job();});
    queue=entry.promise.catch(()=>null);
    return entry.promise;
  }
  function evict(){
    cache.sort((a,b)=>b.last-a.last);
    while(cache.length>MAX_ENTRIES){const i=cache.findIndex((e,idx)=>idx>=MAX_ENTRIES-1&&(e.out||e.dead||e.failed));if(i<0)break;cache.splice(i,1);}
    for(let i=cache.length-1;i>=0;i--){if(cache[i].dead)cache.splice(i,1);}
  }
  function plan(buf,startSec,endSec){
    const total=buf.duration;
    if(total<=FULL_BUFFER_MAX_SEC)return {start:0,end:total};
    return {start:Math.max(0,startSec-PAD_SEC),end:Math.min(total,endSec+PAD_SEC)};
  }
  function find(buf,speed,a,b){
    return cache.find(e=>!e.dead&&!e.failed&&e.src===buf&&Math.abs(e.speed-speed)<.0005&&e.start<=a+.002&&e.end>=b-.002);
  }
  function toResult(e){return {buffer:e.out,originSec:e.originSec};}
  /* Synchronous lookup for the live scheduler: result object when ready, null while computing,
     false when this range cannot be stretched (caller then uses the legacy playbackRate path). */
  function getReady(buf,speed,startSec,endSec){
    if(!buf||!(speed>0))return false;
    const a=Math.max(0,startSec),b=Math.min(buf.duration,Math.max(a+.01,endSec));
    let e=find(buf,speed,a,b);
    if(e){e.last=Date.now();return e.out?toResult(e):null;}
    if(b-a>MAX_RANGE_SEC)return false;
    const p=plan(buf,a,b);
    e={src:buf,speed,start:p.start,end:p.end,out:null,promise:null,last:Date.now(),dead:false,failed:false};
    cache.push(e);evict();enqueue(e,false);
    return null;
  }
  /* Async lookup for export (never cached, never aborted). */
  async function request(buf,speed,startSec,endSec){
    if(!buf||!(speed>0))return null;
    const a=Math.max(0,startSec),b=Math.min(buf.duration,Math.max(a+.01,endSec));
    if(b-a<=0)return null;
    const e={src:buf,speed,start:a,end:b,out:null,last:Date.now(),dead:false,failed:false};
    const r=await enqueue(e,true);
    return r&&r.out?toResult(r):null;
  }
  /* Debounced: pre-compute the stretch for every clip whose speed is not 1x, so playback starts instantly. */
  let prewarmTimer=0;
  function prewarm(resolveBuffer){
    clearTimeout(prewarmTimer);
    prewarmTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
      try{
        if(typeof overlays==='undefined'||!Array.isArray(overlays))return;
        overlays.forEach(ov=>{
          if(!ov||!ov.src||ov.reversePlayback||!['video','audio'].includes(ov.type))return;
          const speed=Number(ov.speed)||1;if(Math.abs(speed-1)<.001)return;
          const buf=resolveBuffer(ov);if(!buf)return;
          const off=Math.max(0,Number(ov.sourceOffset)||0);
          const len=Math.max(0,(Number(ov.trimEnd)||0)-(Number(ov.trimStart)||0))*speed;
          getReady(buf,speed,off,off+len+.1);
        });
      }catch(_e){}
    },350);
  }
  window.ethvellePitchSafeStretch={getReady,request,prewarm,_wsola:wsola};
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v221-media-perspective-js">
/* Ethvelle v221: Perspective for normal media clips (video / image / background), same idea as the text editor's Perspective tool.
   Preview: a CSS "perspective() rotateX() rotateY()" suffix is appended to the clip's transform (innermost, so it tilts the clip in place).
   Export: canvas has no 3D transforms, so the clip is drawn as a small triangle mesh projected with the same maths. */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const LIMIT=55,DIST=900;
  const SUFFIX_RE=/\s*perspective\(\d+(?:\.\d+)?px\)\s*rotateX\([^)]*\)\s*rotateY\([^)]*\)\s*$/;
  const num=v=>{v=Number(v);return Number.isFinite(v)?Math.max(-LIMIT,Math.min(LIMIT,v)):0;};
  window.ethvellePerspectiveSuffix=function(clip){
    const x=num(clip&&clip.mediaPerspectiveX),y=num(clip&&clip.mediaPerspectiveY);
    return (x||y)?` perspective(${DIST}px) rotateX(${y}deg) rotateY(${x}deg)`:'';
  };
  window.ethvelleStripPerspective=function(value){return String(value||'').replace(SUFFIX_RE,'');};
  window.ethvelleMediaPerspectiveDistance=DIST;
  function project(x,y,a,b,d){
    const cb=Math.cos(b),sb=Math.sin(b),ca=Math.cos(a),sa=Math.sin(a);
    const x1=x*cb,z1=-x*sb;
    const y2=y*ca-z1*sa,z2=y*sa+z1*ca;
    const f=d/(d-z2);
    return [x1*f,y2*f];
  }
  /* Draw img's source rect (sx,sy,sw,sh) into dest rect (dx,dy,dw,dh) tilted around (cx,cy); px/py are degrees like the CSS version. */
  window.ethvelleDrawPerspectiveImage=function(ctx,img,sx,sy,sw,sh,dx,dy,dw,dh,px,py,cx,cy,dist){
    const a=num(py)*Math.PI/180,b=num(px)*Math.PI/180,d=Math.max(50,Number(dist)||DIST);
    const N=12,M=12,V=[];
    for(let j=0;j<=M;j++){
      for(let i=0;i<=N;i++){
        const u=i/N,v=j/M;
        const p=project(dx+u*dw-cx,dy+v*dh-cy,a,b,d);
        V.push({X:cx+p[0],Y:cy+p[1],S:sx+u*sw,T:sy+v*sh});
      }
    }
    const at=(i,j)=>V[j*(N+1)+i];
    function tri(p0,p1,p2){
      const det=(p1.S-p0.S)*(p2.T-p0.T)-(p2.S-p0.S)*(p1.T-p0.T);
      if(!det)return;
      const A=((p1.X-p0.X)*(p2.T-p0.T)-(p2.X-p0.X)*(p1.T-p0.T))/det;
      const B=((p1.Y-p0.Y)*(p2.T-p0.T)-(p2.Y-p0.Y)*(p1.T-p0.T))/det;
      const C=((p2.X-p0.X)*(p1.S-p0.S)-(p1.X-p0.X)*(p2.S-p0.S))/det;
      const D=((p2.Y-p0.Y)*(p1.S-p0.S)-(p1.Y-p0.Y)*(p2.S-p0.S))/det;
      const E=p0.X-A*p0.S-C*p0.T,F=p0.Y-B*p0.S-D*p0.T;
      const gx=(p0.X+p1.X+p2.X)/3,gy=(p0.Y+p1.Y+p2.Y)/3;
      const grow=pt=>{const vx=pt.X-gx,vy=pt.Y-gy,l=Math.hypot(vx,vy)||1;return [pt.X+vx/l*.7,pt.Y+vy/l*.7];};
      const g0=grow(p0),g1=grow(p1),g2=grow(p2);
      const minS=Math.min(p0.S,p1.S,p2.S),maxS=Math.max(p0.S,p1.S,p2.S),minT=Math.min(p0.T,p1.T,p2.T),maxT=Math.max(p0.T,p1.T,p2.T);
      const bx=Math.max(0,Math.floor(minS)),by=Math.max(0,Math.floor(minT));
      const bw=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(maxS)-bx+1),bh=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(maxT)-by+1);
      ctx.save();
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(g0[0],g0[1]);ctx.lineTo(g1[0],g1[1]);ctx.lineTo(g2[0],g2[1]);ctx.closePath();ctx.clip();
      ctx.transform(A,B,C,D,E,F);
      try{ctx.drawImage(img,bx,by,bw,bh,bx,by,bw,bh);}catch(_e){}
      ctx.restore();
    }
    for(let j=0;j<M;j++){
      for(let i=0;i<N;i++){
        const p00=at(i,j),p10=at(i+1,j),p01=at(i,j+1),p11=at(i+1,j+1);
        tri(p00,p10,p01);tri(p10,p11,p01);
      }
    }
  };
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v187-all-text-opacity-bend-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const allOv=()=>{try{return Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:(window.overlays||[]);}catch(_e){return window.overlays||[];}};
  const texts=()=>allOv().filter(o=>o&&o.type==='text');
  function hostFor(o){
    let h=null;try{h=overlayMediaEls&&overlayMediaEls[o.id];}catch(_e){}
    if(!h){const l=document.getElementById('overlayMediaLayer');if(l)h=[...l.children].find(n=>String(n.dataset.overlayId||n.dataset.ovId||'')===String(o.id))||null;}
    return h;
  }
  /* ---- Opacity tab: one slider fades EVERY text layer (fill, stroke, shadow and background plate together). ---- */
  const prevSet=window.ethvellePicsartSetValue;
  if(typeof prevSet==='function'&&!prevSet.__ev187){
    const wrapped=function(key,value,unit,live){
      const out=prevSet.apply(this,arguments);
      if(key==='textOverallOpacity'){
        const v=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(value)));
        if(Number.isFinite(v)){
          texts().forEach(o=>{
            o.textOverallOpacity=v;
            const h=hostFor(o);if(h)h.style.opacity=String(v/100);
          });
          if(!live){
            try{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();}catch(_e){}
            try{if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();}catch(_e){}
          }
        }
      }
      requestAnimationFrame(fixBend);
      return out;
    };
    wrapped.__ev187=true;window.ethvellePicsartSetValue=wrapped;
  }
  /* ---- Bend: draw bent letters with plain 2D transforms (3D layers made letters vanish on Android). ---- */
  function fixBend(){
    try{
      texts().forEach(o=>{
        const h=hostFor(o);if(!h||!h.querySelector)return;
        const node=h.querySelector('.ethvelle-overlay-text-content');if(!node)return;
        const g=node.querySelector(':scope > .evpa-text-effect-layer');
        if(!g||!g.dataset.evpaBendRaw||!g.children.length)return;
        const num=v=>Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Number(v)||0));
        const up=num(o.textBendUp),down=num(o.textBendDown),front=num(o.textBendFront),back=num(o.textBendBack);
        const legacy=Number(o.textCurve)||0;
        const curve=Math.max(-100,Math.min(100,(up||down)?(down-up):(legacy*(o.textCurveReverse?-1:1))));
        const spans=Array.from(g.children).filter(sp=>sp.tagName==='SPAN');
        const den=Math.max(1,spans.length-1);
        const needs3d=front!==back;
        node.style.setProperty('transform-style','flat','important');
        node.style.setProperty('will-change','auto','important');
        node.style.setProperty('backface-visibility','visible','important');
        node.style.setProperty('-webkit-backface-visibility','visible','important');
        g.style.setProperty('transform-style','flat','important');
        g.style.setProperty('visibility','visible','important');
        spans.forEach((sp,i)=>{
          const x=i/den-.5,y=x*x*curve*.45,rot=x*curve*.42,rx=x*x*(back-front)*.52,ry=x*(front-back)*.72;
          let t=`translateY(${y}px) rotate(${rot}deg)`;
          if(needs3d)t=`perspective(700px) ${t} rotateX(${rx}deg) rotateY(${ry}deg)`;
          if(sp.style.transform!==t)sp.style.transform=t;
          sp.style.setProperty('visibility','visible','important');
          sp.style.setProperty('opacity','1','important');
          sp.style.setProperty('backface-visibility','visible','important');
          sp.style.setProperty('-webkit-backface-visibility','visible','important');
        });
      });
    }catch(_e){}
  }
  ['renderFrame','renderOverlayLayers'].forEach(name=>{
    const orig=window[name];if(typeof orig!=='function'||orig.__ev187)return;
    const w=function(){const r=orig.apply(this,arguments);fixBend();return r;};
    w.__ev187=true;window[name]=w;
  });
})();
</script>

<style id="ethvelle-v193-palette-settle-css">
  /* v193: while Font/Color is opening, the swatch row is rebuilt several times in ~40ms by the stacked
